# 📦 LUNA - TÜM KODLAR (TEMİZ, EKSİKSİZ, SON HAL)

Tüm 15 düzeltmeyi uyguladığım **final** kodları parça parça veriyorum.

**Plan:**
- **Part 1/7:** Root build + App modülü (14 dosya)
- **Part 2/7:** core/utils + core/database (9 dosya)
- **Part 3/7:** core/data (17 dosya)
- **Part 4/7:** core/ui (15 dosya)
- **Part 5/7:** feature/fortune + feature/home (11 dosya)
- **Part 6/7:** feature/social + profile + onboarding (9 dosya)
- **Part 7/7:** Resources + CI/CD + Python (15 dosya)

```proguard
# Luna ProGuard Rules

# Kotlin
-keep class kotlin.Metadata { *; }
-dontwarn kotlin.reflect.jvm.internal.**

# Kotlin Coroutines
-keepnames class kotlinx.coroutines.internal.MainDispatcherFactory {}
-keepnames class kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineExceptionHandler {}
-keepclassmembers class kotlinx.coroutines.** {
    volatile <fields>;
}

# Hilt / Dagger
-dontwarn dagger.hilt.internal.aggregatedroot.codegen.**
-keep class dagger.hilt.** { *; }

-keep class * extends dagger.hilt.android.internal.managers.ViewComponentManager$FragmentContextWrapper { *; }

# Firebase

-keep class com.google.android.gms.** { *; }

# Firestore DTOs
-keep class com.cynora.luna.core.data.remote.** { *; }
-keepclassmembers class com.cynora.luna.core.data.remote.** {
    <init>(...);
    *;
}

# Domain Models
-keep class com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.** { *; }

# Repository Data Classes
-keep class com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.** { *; }

# Enums
-keepclassmembers enum * {
    public static **[] values();
    public static ** valueOf(java.lang.String);
}

# Room
-keep class * extends androidx.room.RoomDatabase
-keep @androidx.room.Entity class *
-dontwarn androidx.room.paging.**

# Compose
-dontwarn androidx.compose.**

# AdMob
-keep public class com.google.android.gms.ads.** { public *; }

# Timber
-dontwarn org.jetbrains.annotations.**

# Parcelable
-keepclassmembers class * implements android.os.Parcelable {
    static ** CREATOR;
}

# Crashlytics

-keep public class * extends java.lang.Exception

# WorkManager + HiltWorker
-keep class com.cynora.luna.work.** { *; }
-keep class androidx.work.** { *; }
-keep class androidx.hilt.work.** { *; }
-keep @androidx.hilt.work.HiltWorker class * { *; }

# General
-keepattributes Signature
-keepattributes Exceptions
-keepattributes *Annotation*
```

## 📄 8. `LunaApplication.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna

import android.app.Application
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.os.Build
import androidx.hilt.work.HiltWorkerFactory
import androidx.work.Configuration
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.seed.SeedDataLoader
import com.cynora.luna.work.WorkScheduler
import com.google.android.gms.ads.MobileAds
import com.google.android.gms.ads.RequestConfiguration
import com.google.android.ump.ConsentInformation
import com.google.android.ump.ConsentRequestParameters
import com.google.android.ump.UserMessagingPlatform
import dagger.hilt.android.HiltAndroidApp
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import timber.log.Timber
import javax.inject.Inject

@HiltAndroidApp
class LunaApplication : Application(), Configuration.Provider {

    @Inject
    lateinit var seedDataLoader: SeedDataLoader

    @Inject
    lateinit var workerFactory: HiltWorkerFactory

    private val appScope = CoroutineScope(Job() + Dispatchers.IO)
    private lateinit var consentInfo: ConsentInformation

    override val workManagerConfiguration: Configuration
        get() = Configuration.Builder()
            .setWorkerFactory(workerFactory)
            .build()

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()

        if (BuildConfig.DEBUG) {
            Timber.plant(Timber.DebugTree())
            MobileAds.setRequestConfiguration(
                RequestConfiguration.Builder()
                    .setTestDeviceIds(listOf("EMULATOR_ID"))
                    .build()
            )
        }

        createNotificationChannels()
        initConsent()

        appScope.launch {
            try {
                seedDataLoader.loadSeedDataIfNeeded()
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Timber.e(e, "Seed data load failed")
            }
        }

        WorkScheduler.scheduleDailyFortune(this, 9, 0)
    }

    private fun initAdsIfAllowed() {
        val info = UserMessagingPlatform.getConsentInformation(this)
        if (info.canRequestAds()) {
            MobileAds.initialize(this) { status ->
                Timber.d("AdMob initialized: $status")
            }
        }
    }

    private fun initConsent() {
        val info = UserMessagingPlatform.getConsentInformation(this)
        consentInfo = info

        if (info.canRequestAds()) {
            initAdsIfAllowed()
        }

        info.requestConsentInfoUpdate(
            this,
            ConsentRequestParameters.Builder()
                .setTagForUnderAgeOfConsent(false)
                .build(),
            {
                if (info.isConsentFormAvailable) {
                    loadConsentForm()
                }
            },
            { error ->
                Timber.e("Consent update failed: ${error.message}")
            }
        )
    }

    private fun loadConsentForm() {
        UserMessagingPlatform.loadConsentForm(
            this,
            { form ->
                if (consentInfo.consentStatus == ConsentInformation.ConsentStatus.REQUIRED) {
                    form.show(this) { error ->
                        if (error != null) {
                            Timber.e("Consent form show failed: ${error.message}")
                        }
                        initAdsIfAllowed()
                    }
                } else {
                    initAdsIfAllowed()
                }
            },
            { error ->
                Timber.e("Consent form load failed: ${error.message}")
            }
        )
    }

    private fun createNotificationChannels() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val nm = getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)

            val channels = listOf(
                NotificationChannel(
                    "luna_daily_fortune",
                    "Daily Fortune",
                    NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT
                ).apply {
                    description = "Daily fortune readings"
                    enableLights(true)
                    enableVibration(true)
                    lightColor = 0xFFD4AF37.toInt()
                },
                NotificationChannel(
                    "luna_social",
                    "Community",
                    NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_LOW
                ).apply {
                    description = "Community interactions"
                },
                NotificationChannel(
                    "luna_calendar",
                    "Mystic Calendar",
                    NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT
                ).apply {
                    description = "Moon phases"
                    enableLights(true)
                    lightColor = 0xFF9D76C1.toInt()
                },
                NotificationChannel(
                    "luna_special",
                    "Special Events",
                    NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_HIGH
                ).apply {
                    description = "Cosmic events"
                    enableVibration(true)
                }
            )

            nm.createNotificationChannels(channels)
        }
    }
}
```

## 📄 9. `MainActivity.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna

import android.os.Bundle
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.compose.material3.Surface
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.datastore.UserPreferencesDataStore
import com.cynora.luna.core.ui.theme.LunaTheme
import com.cynora.luna.navigation.LunaNavHost
import dagger.hilt.android.AndroidEntryPoint
import javax.inject.Inject

@AndroidEntryPoint
class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {

    @Inject
    lateinit var dataStore: UserPreferencesDataStore

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        enableEdgeToEdge()
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)

        setContent {
            val isDark by dataStore.isDarkModeEnabled()
                .collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = true)

            LunaTheme(darkTheme = isDark) {
                Surface(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
                    LunaNavHost()
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 📄 10. `LunaNavHost.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.navigation

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.padding
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Favorite
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Home
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Person
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Star
import androidx.compose.material3.Scaffold
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.hilt.navigation.compose.hiltViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import androidx.navigation.NavDestination.Companion.hierarchy
import androidx.navigation.NavGraph.Companion.findStartDestination
import androidx.navigation.compose.NavHost
import androidx.navigation.compose.composable
import androidx.navigation.compose.currentBackStackEntryAsState
import androidx.navigation.compose.rememberNavController
import androidx.navigation.navArgument
import androidx.navigation.navDeepLink
import com.cynora.luna.core.ui.components.BottomNavItem
import com.cynora.luna.core.ui.components.LunaBottomNavigation
import com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.FortuneScreen
import com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.affirmations.AffirmationsScreen
import com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.angelnumbers.AngelNumbersScreen
import com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.birthchart.BirthChartScreen
import com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.compatibility.CompatibilityScreen
import com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.dailycard.DailyCardScreen
import com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.luckynumbers.LuckyNumbersScreen
import com.cynora.luna.feature.home.HomeScreen
import com.cynora.luna.feature.onboarding.OnboardingScreen
import com.cynora.luna.feature.profile.ProfileScreen
import com.cynora.luna.feature.social.SocialScreen

object Routes {
    const val HOME = "home"
    const val FORTUNE = "fortune"
    const val SOCIAL = "social"
    const val PROFILE = "profile"
    const val BIRTH_CHART = "birth_chart"
    const val DAILY_CARD = "daily_card"
    const val COMPATIBILITY = "compatibility"
    const val ANGEL_NUMBERS = "angel_numbers"
    const val LUCKY_NUMBERS = "lucky_numbers"
    const val AFFIRMATIONS = "affirmations"
}

@Composable
fun LunaNavHost(appVM: AppViewModel = hiltViewModel()) {
    val nav = rememberNavController()
    val appState by appVM.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()

    val items = listOf(
        BottomNavItem(Routes.HOME, Icons.Default.Home, "Home"),
        BottomNavItem(Routes.FORTUNE, Icons.Default.Star, "Fortune"),
        BottomNavItem(Routes.SOCIAL, Icons.Default.Favorite, "Community"),
        BottomNavItem(Routes.PROFILE, Icons.Default.Person, "Profile")
    )

    val backStack by nav.currentBackStackEntryAsState()
    val currentRoute = backStack?.destination?.route
    val showBottom = currentRoute in listOf(
        Routes.HOME, Routes.FORTUNE, Routes.SOCIAL, Routes.PROFILE
    )

    if (appState.isOnboardingComplete) {
        Scaffold(
            bottomBar = {
                if (showBottom) {
                    LunaBottomNavigation(items, currentRoute) { item ->
                        nav.navigate(item.route) {
                            popUpTo(nav.graph.findStartDestination().id) {
                                saveState = true
                            }
                            launchSingleTop = true
                            restoreState = true
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        ) { pad ->
            NavHost(nav, Routes.HOME, Modifier.padding(pad)) {
                composable(Routes.HOME) {
                    HomeScreen(
                        onNavigateToFortune = { nav.navigate(Routes.FORTUNE) },
                        onNavigateToBirthChart = { nav.navigate(Routes.BIRTH_CHART) },
                        onNavigateToDailyCard = { nav.navigate(Routes.DAILY_CARD) },
                        onNavigateToCompatibility = { nav.navigate(Routes.COMPATIBILITY) },
                        onNavigateToAngelNumbers = { nav.navigate(Routes.ANGEL_NUMBERS) },
                        onNavigateToLuckyNumbers = { nav.navigate(Routes.LUCKY_NUMBERS) },
                        onNavigateToAffirmations = { nav.navigate(Routes.AFFIRMATIONS) }
                    )
                }
                composable(Routes.FORTUNE) { FortuneScreen() }
                composable(Routes.SOCIAL) { SocialScreen() }
                composable(Routes.PROFILE) { ProfileScreen() }
                composable(Routes.BIRTH_CHART) {
                    BirthChartScreen { nav.popBackStack() }
                }
                composable(Routes.DAILY_CARD) {
                    DailyCardScreen { nav.popBackStack() }
                }
                composable(Routes.COMPATIBILITY) {
                    CompatibilityScreen { nav.popBackStack() }
                }
                composable(Routes.ANGEL_NUMBERS) {
                    AngelNumbersScreen { nav.popBackStack() }
                }
                composable(Routes.LUCKY_NUMBERS) {
                    LuckyNumbersScreen { nav.popBackStack() }
                }
                composable(Routes.AFFIRMATIONS) {
                    AffirmationsScreen { nav.popBackStack() }
                }
                composable(
                    route = "${Routes.FORTUNE}/{fortuneId}",
                    deepLinks = listOf(
                        navDeepLink { uriPattern = "https://luna.cynora.com/fortune/{fortuneId}" }
                    ),
                    arguments = listOf(navArgument("fortuneId") { defaultValue = "" })
                ) { backStackEntry ->
                    val fortuneId = backStackEntry.arguments?.getString("fortuneId")
                    FortuneScreen(initialFortuneId = fortuneId)
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        OnboardingScreen {
            appVM.onIntent(AppIntent.CompleteOnboarding)
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 📄 11. `AppViewModel.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.navigation

import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.datastore.UserPreferencesDataStore
import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import javax.inject.Inject

@Immutable
data class AppState(
    val isOnboardingComplete: Boolean = false
)

sealed interface AppIntent {
    data object CompleteOnboarding : AppIntent
}

@HiltViewModel
class AppViewModel @Inject constructor(
    private val dataStore: UserPreferencesDataStore
) : ViewModel() {

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(AppState())
    val state: StateFlow<AppState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    init {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            dataStore.isOnboardingComplete().collect { isComplete ->
                _state.update { it.copy(isOnboardingComplete = isComplete) }
            }
        }
    }

    fun onIntent(intent: AppIntent) {
        when (intent) {
            is AppIntent.CompleteOnboarding -> {
                viewModelScope.launch {
                    dataStore.setOnboardingComplete(true)
                    _state.update { it.copy(isOnboardingComplete = true) }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## 📄 13. `DatabaseModule.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.di

import android.content.Context
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.room.migration.Migration
import androidx.sqlite.db.SupportSQLiteDatabase
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.LunaDatabase
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.dao.FortuneDao
import dagger.Module
import dagger.Provides
import dagger.hilt.InstallIn
import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
import dagger.hilt.components.SingletonComponent
import javax.inject.Singleton

@Module
@InstallIn(SingletonComponent::class)
object DatabaseModule {

    @Provides
    @Singleton
    fun provideDatabase(@ApplicationContext context: Context): LunaDatabase {
        return Room.databaseBuilder(
            context,
            LunaDatabase::class.java,
            "luna_db"
        )
            .addMigrations(MIGRATION_1_2)
            .fallbackToDestructiveMigrationOnDowngrade()
            .build()
    }

    @Provides
    @Singleton
    fun provideFortuneDao(database: LunaDatabase): FortuneDao {
        return database.fortuneDao()
    }
}

val MIGRATION_1_2 = object : Migration(1, 2) {
    override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
        // Schema değişikliği yok
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.utils

import android.app.Activity
import android.content.Context
import android.content.ContextWrapper

/**
 * Compose context'ten güvenli Activity bulma
 * ✅ tailrec ile optimize edildi (infinite loop riski yok)
 */
tailrec fun Context.findActivity(): Activity? = when (this) {
    is Activity -> this
    is ContextWrapper -> baseContext.findActivity()
    else -> null
}
```

### 📄 3. `core/utils/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/utils/ShareUtils.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.utils

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import androidx.core.content.FileProvider
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream

/**
 * Global paylaşım utility'si
 * ✅ FileProvider entegrasyonu
 */
object ShareUtils {

    fun shareFortune(
        context: Context,
        fortuneText: String,
        dailyIntention: String,
        fortuneType: String,
        shareImage: Bitmap? = null
    ) {
        val shareText = buildString {
            appendLine("✨ $fortuneType by Luna ✨")
            appendLine()
            appendLine("\"$fortuneText\"")
            appendLine()
            appendLine("🌟 Daily Intention: $dailyIntention")
            appendLine()
            appendLine("— Get Luna: Your Personal Cosmic Guide")
            appendLine("https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.cynora.luna")
        }

        val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
            type = if (shareImage != null) "image/*" else "text/plain"
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, shareText)

            shareImage?.let { bitmap ->
                val uri = saveBitmapToCache(context, bitmap, "luna_fortune.png")
                putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uri)
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
            }
        }

        context.startActivity(
            Intent.createChooser(intent, "Share via").apply {
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
            }
        )
    }

    fun shareIntention(context: Context, intention: String) {
        val shareText = buildString {
            appendLine("🌟 Today's Intention:")
            appendLine()
            appendLine("\"$intention\"")
            appendLine()
            appendLine("— Luna")
            appendLine("https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.cynora.luna")
        }

        val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
            type = "text/plain"
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, shareText)
        }

        context.startActivity(
            Intent.createChooser(intent, "Share intention").apply {
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
            }
        )
    }

    private fun saveBitmapToCache(context: Context, bitmap: Bitmap, fileName: String): android.net.Uri {
        val cachePath = File(context.cacheDir, "images")
        cachePath.mkdirs()
        val file = File(cachePath, fileName)
        FileOutputStream(file).use { stream ->
            bitmap.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.PNG, 100, stream)
        }
        return FileProvider.getUriForFile(
            context,
            "${context.packageName}.fileprovider",
            file
        )
    }
}
```

### 📄 4. `core/utils/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/utils/HapticUtils.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.utils

import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.hapticfeedback.HapticFeedback
import androidx.compose.ui.hapticfeedback.HapticFeedbackType
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalHapticFeedback

/**
 * Premium haptic feedback manager
 * ✅ Watermelon UI esintili mikro-etkileşimler
 */
@Composable
fun rememberHapticFeedback(): HapticFeedbackManager {
    val haptic = LocalHapticFeedback.current
    return HapticFeedbackManager(haptic)
}

class HapticFeedbackManager(private val haptic: HapticFeedback) {
    
    fun buttonClick() {
        haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.LongPress)
    }
    
    fun toggle() {
        haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.TextHandleMove)
    }
    
    fun success() {
        haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.LongPress)
    }
    
    fun error() {
        haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.TextHandleMove)
    }
    
    fun selection() {
        haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.KeyboardPress)
    }
}
```

### 📄 5. `core/utils/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/utils/PermissionManager.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.utils

import android.Manifest
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat

/**
 * 2026+ Premium Permission Manager
 * - Version-aware permission checks
 * - Graceful degradation
 * - Permission state tracking
 */
object PermissionManager {
    
    fun hasNotificationPermission(context: Context): Boolean {
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
                context,
                Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS
            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        } else {
            true // Android 12 ve altı otomatik izinli
        }
    }
    
    fun hasCameraPermission(context: Context): Boolean {
        return ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
            context,
            Manifest.permission.CAMERA
        ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
    }
    
    fun hasGalleryPermission(context: Context): Boolean {
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
                context,
                Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_IMAGES
            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        } else {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
                context,
                Manifest.permission.READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE
            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        }
    }
    
    fun hasBiometricPermission(context: Context): Boolean {
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
                context,
                Manifest.permission.USE_BIOMETRIC
            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        } else {
            true
        }
    }
    
    fun hasExactAlarmPermission(context: Context): Boolean {
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            val alarmManager = context.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE) as android.app.AlarmManager
            alarmManager.canScheduleExactAlarms()
        } else {
            true
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Tüm gerekli izinlerin durumunu döndürür
     */
    fun getAllPermissionStatus(context: Context): Map<String, Boolean> {
        return mapOf(
            "notifications" to hasNotificationPermission(context),
            "camera" to hasCameraPermission(context),
            "gallery" to hasGalleryPermission(context),
            "biometric" to hasBiometricPermission(context),
            "exact_alarm" to hasExactAlarmPermission(context)
        )
    }
}
```

### 📄 7. `core/database/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/database/LunaDatabase.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.database

import androidx.room.Database
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase
import androidx.room.TypeConverters
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.dao.FortuneDao
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.entity.FortuneConverters
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.entity.FortuneEntity

@Database(
    entities = [FortuneEntity::class],
    version = 2,
    exportSchema = false
)
@TypeConverters(FortuneConverters::class)
abstract class LunaDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
    abstract fun fortuneDao(): FortuneDao

    companion object {
        const val DATABASE_NAME = "luna_db"
    }
}
```

### 📄 8. `core/database/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/database/entity/FortuneEntity.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.database.entity

import androidx.room.Entity
import androidx.room.PrimaryKey
import androidx.room.TypeConverter
import androidx.room.TypeConverters

@Entity(tableName = "fortunes")
@TypeConverters(FortuneConverters::class)
data class FortuneEntity(
    @PrimaryKey val id: String,
    val type: String,
    val text: String,
    val dailyIntention: String,
    val moodTags: List<String>,
    val focusTags: List<String>,
    val keywordTags: List<String>,
    val rarity: String,
    val createdAt: Long,
    val isSeen: Boolean = false
)

class FortuneConverters {
    @TypeConverter
    fun fromStringList(value: List<String>): String = value.joinToString(",")

    @TypeConverter
    fun toStringList(value: String): List<String> =
        if (value.isBlank()) emptyList() else value.split(",")
}
```

### 📄 9. `core/database/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/database/dao/FortuneDao.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.database.dao

import androidx.room.Dao
import androidx.room.Insert
import androidx.room.OnConflictStrategy
import androidx.room.Query
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.entity.FortuneEntity
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

@Dao
interface FortuneDao {

    @Query("SELECT * FROM fortunes")
    fun getAll(): Flow<List<FortuneEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM fortunes WHERE type = :typeId")
    fun getByType(typeId: String): Flow<List<FortuneEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM fortunes WHERE id = :fortuneId")
    suspend fun getById(fortuneId: String): FortuneEntity?

    @Query("UPDATE fortunes SET isSeen = 1 WHERE id = :fortuneId")
    suspend fun markSeen(fortuneId: String)

    @Query("UPDATE fortunes SET isSeen = 0")
    suspend fun resetSeenStatus()

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertAll(fortunes: List<FortuneEntity>)

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insert(fortune: FortuneEntity)

    @Query("DELETE FROM fortunes")
    suspend fun deleteAll()

    @Query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM fortunes")
    suspend fun count(): Int
}
```

### 📄 10. `core/database/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/database/datastore/UserPreferencesDataStore.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.database.datastore

import android.content.Context
import androidx.datastore.core.DataStore
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.Preferences
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.booleanPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.edit
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.intPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.stringPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.preferencesDataStore
import com.google.gson.Gson
import com.google.gson.reflect.TypeToken
import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

private val Context.dataStore: DataStore<Preferences> by preferencesDataStore(name = "luna_preferences")

@Singleton
class UserPreferencesDataStore @Inject constructor(
    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context
) {
    private val dataStore = context.dataStore
    private val gson = Gson()

    companion object {
        val SEEN_FORTUNE_IDS_KEY = stringPreferencesKey("seen_fortune_ids_json")
        val USER_ZODIAC_KEY = stringPreferencesKey("user_zodiac")
        val USER_ID_KEY = stringPreferencesKey("user_id")
        val ONBOARDING_COMPLETE_KEY = booleanPreferencesKey("onboarding_complete")
        val NOTIFICATIONS_ENABLED_KEY = booleanPreferencesKey("notifications_enabled")
        val DARK_MODE_ENABLED_KEY = booleanPreferencesKey("dark_mode_enabled")
        val FORTUNE_COUNT_KEY = intPreferencesKey("fortune_count")
        val STREAK_COUNT_KEY = intPreferencesKey("streak_count")
        val LAST_FORTUNE_DATE_KEY = stringPreferencesKey("last_fortune_date")
    }

    // --- Seen List (JSON ile sıralama korunur) ---
    fun getSeenFortuneIds(): Flow<Set<String>> {
        return dataStore.data.map { prefs ->
            val json = prefs[SEEN_FORTUNE_IDS_KEY] ?: "[]"
            try {
                val type = object : TypeToken<List<String>>() {}.type
                val list: List<String> = gson.fromJson(json, type)
                list.toSet()
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                emptySet()
            }
        }
    }

    suspend fun updateSeenFortuneIds(fortuneId: String) {
        dataStore.edit { prefs ->
            val currentJson = prefs[SEEN_FORTUNE_IDS_KEY] ?: "[]"
            val current: MutableList<String> = try {
                val type = object : TypeToken<List<String>>() {}.type
                gson.fromJson(currentJson, type)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                mutableListOf()
            }
            
            // Eski varsa kaldır (en sona taşımak için)
            current.remove(fortuneId)
            current.add(fortuneId)
            
            // Limit: 500 fal. Aşılırsa eski yarısını sil
            if (current.size > 500) {
                val trimmed = current.takeLast(250)
                current.clear()
                current.addAll(trimmed)
            }
            
            prefs[SEEN_FORTUNE_IDS_KEY] = gson.toJson(current)
        }
    }

    suspend fun resetSeenFortuneIds() {
        dataStore.edit { it[SEEN_FORTUNE_IDS_KEY] = "[]" }
    }

    // --- User ID (kalıcı kimlik) ---
    suspend fun getOrCreateUserId(): String {
        var result = ""
        dataStore.edit { prefs ->
            val existing = prefs[USER_ID_KEY]
            if (existing != null) {
                result = existing
            } else {
                val generated = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString()
                prefs[USER_ID_KEY] = generated
                result = generated
            }
        }
        return result
    }

    // --- Zodiac ---
    suspend fun saveUserZodiac(zodiacName: String) {
        dataStore.edit { it[USER_ZODIAC_KEY] = zodiacName }
    }

    fun getUserZodiac(): Flow<String?> {
        return dataStore.data.map { it[USER_ZODIAC_KEY] }
    }

    // --- Onboarding ---
    suspend fun setOnboardingComplete(complete: Boolean) {
        dataStore.edit { it[ONBOARDING_COMPLETE_KEY] = complete }
    }

    fun isOnboardingComplete(): Flow<Boolean> {
        return dataStore.data.map { it[ONBOARDING_COMPLETE_KEY] ?: false }
    }

    // --- Notifications ---
    suspend fun setNotificationsEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        dataStore.edit { it[NOTIFICATIONS_ENABLED_KEY] = enabled }
    }

    fun areNotificationsEnabled(): Flow<Boolean> {
        return dataStore.data.map { it[NOTIFICATIONS_ENABLED_KEY] ?: true }
    }

    // --- Dark Mode ---
    suspend fun setDarkModeEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        dataStore.edit { it[DARK_MODE_ENABLED_KEY] = enabled }
    }

    fun isDarkModeEnabled(): Flow<Boolean> {
        return dataStore.data.map { it[DARK_MODE_ENABLED_KEY] ?: true }
    }

    // --- Stats ---
    suspend fun incrementFortuneCount() {
        dataStore.edit { prefs ->
            val current = prefs[FORTUNE_COUNT_KEY] ?: 0
            prefs[FORTUNE_COUNT_KEY] = current + 1
        }
    }

    fun getFortuneCount(): Flow<Int> {
        return dataStore.data.map { it[FORTUNE_COUNT_KEY] ?: 0 }
    }

    fun getStreakCount(): Flow<Int> {
        return dataStore.data.map { it[STREAK_COUNT_KEY] ?: 0 }
    }

    suspend fun updateStreak(todayDate: String) {
        dataStore.edit { prefs ->
            val lastDate = prefs[LAST_FORTUNE_DATE_KEY]
            val currentStreak = prefs[STREAK_COUNT_KEY] ?: 0
            if (lastDate != todayDate) {
                prefs[STREAK_COUNT_KEY] = currentStreak + 1
                prefs[LAST_FORTUNE_DATE_KEY] = todayDate
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### 📄 2. `core/data/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/data/model/Fortune.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.data.model

import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable

@Immutable
enum class FortuneType(val id: String, val displayName: String, val icon: String) {
    COFFEE("coffee", "Coffee Reading", "☕"),
    TAROT("tarot", "Tarot", "🎴"),
    LENORMAND("lenormand", "Lenormand", "🃏"),
    ANGEL_CARDS("angel", "Angel Cards", "👼"),
    DAILY_HOROSCOPE("horoscope", "Horoscope", "⭐"),
    DREAM("dream", "Dream Interpretation", "💭"),
    PALMISTRY("palm", "Palmistry", "✋"),
    LOVE("love", "Love Fortune", "💕"),
    NUMEROLOGY("numerology", "Numerology", "🔢"),
    COMBINATION("combo", "Combination", "✨"),
    BIRTH_CHART("birth_chart", "Birth Chart", "🌌"),
    ZODIAC_COMPATIBILITY("compatibility", "Compatibility", "💑"),
    DAILY_CARD("daily_card", "Daily Card", "🎯"),
    ANGEL_NUMBERS("angel_numbers", "Angel Numbers", "🔮"),
    AFFIRMATION("affirmation", "Affirmations", "💫"),
    LUCKY_NUMBERS("lucky_numbers", "Lucky Numbers", "🍀");

    companion object {
        fun fromId(id: String): FortuneType {
            return entries.find { it.id == id } ?: COFFEE
        }
    }
}

@Immutable
enum class Mood(val id: String, val displayName: String) {
    STRESSED("stressed", "Stressed"),
    HOPEFUL("hopeful", "Hopeful"),
    CONFUSED("confused", "Confused"),
    EXCITED("excited", "Excited"),
    TIRED("tired", "Tired"),
    PEACEFUL("peaceful", "Peaceful");

    companion object {
        fun fromId(id: String): Mood {
            return entries.find { it.id == id } ?: HOPEFUL
        }
    }
}

@Immutable
enum class FocusArea(val id: String, val displayName: String) {
    LOVE("love", "Love & Relationships"),
    CAREER("career", "Career & Work"),
    MONEY("money", "Money & Abundance"),
    HEALTH("health", "Health & Energy"),
    GENERAL("general", "General Energy"),
    SPIRITUAL("spiritual", "Spiritual Growth");

    companion object {
        fun fromId(id: String): FocusArea {
            return entries.find { it.id == id } ?: GENERAL
        }
    }
}

@Immutable
enum class Rarity(val id: String, val displayName: String, val colorHex: Long) {
    COMMON("common", "Common", 0xFF9E9E9E),
    RARE("rare", "Rare", 0xFF2196F3),
    EPIC("epic", "Epic", 0xFF9C27B0),
    LEGENDARY("legendary", "Legendary", 0xFFFFD700);

    companion object {
        fun fromId(id: String): Rarity {
            return entries.find { it.id == id } ?: COMMON
        }
    }
}

@Immutable
data class Fortune(
    val id: String,
    val type: FortuneType,
    val text: String,
    val dailyIntention: String,
    val moodTags: List<Mood>,
    val focusTags: List<FocusArea>,
    val keywordTags: List<String>,
    val rarity: Rarity,
    val createdAt: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
) {
    fun calculateMatchScore(userMood: Mood, userFocus: FocusArea): Int {
        var score = 0
        if (moodTags.contains(userMood)) score += 40
        if (focusTags.contains(userFocus)) score += 40
        score += when (rarity) {
            Rarity.COMMON -> 5
            Rarity.RARE -> 10
            Rarity.EPIC -> 15
            Rarity.LEGENDARY -> 20
        }
        return score
    }
}
```

### 📄 5. `core/data/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/data/mapper/FortuneMapper.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.data.mapper

import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.FocusArea
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.Fortune
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.FortuneType
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.Mood
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.Rarity
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.remote.FortuneDto
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.seed.SeedFortuneDto
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.entity.FortuneEntity
import timber.log.Timber

fun FortuneDto.toDomain(): Fortune {
    val validMoods = moodTags.mapNotNull { id ->
        Mood.fromId(id).takeIf { it.id == id } ?: run {
            Timber.w("Invalid mood ID: $id")
            null
        }
    }
    
    val validFocus = focusTags.mapNotNull { id ->
        FocusArea.fromId(id).takeIf { it.id == id } ?: run {
            Timber.w("Invalid focus ID: $id")
            null
        }
    }

    return Fortune(
        id = id,
        type = FortuneType.fromId(type),
        text = text.ifBlank { "The universe has a message for you..." },
        dailyIntention = dailyIntention.ifBlank { "Today I choose peace." },
        moodTags = validMoods,
        focusTags = validFocus,
        keywordTags = keywordTags,
        rarity = Rarity.fromId(rarity),
        createdAt = createdAt
    )
}

fun Fortune.toEntity(): FortuneEntity {
    return FortuneEntity(
        id = id,
        type = type.id,
        text = text,
        dailyIntention = dailyIntention,
        moodTags = moodTags.map { it.id },
        focusTags = focusTags.map { it.id },
        keywordTags = keywordTags,
        rarity = rarity.id,
        createdAt = createdAt,
        isSeen = false
    )
}

fun FortuneEntity.toDomain(): Fortune {
    return Fortune(
        id = id,
        type = FortuneType.fromId(type),
        text = text,
        dailyIntention = dailyIntention,
        moodTags = moodTags.mapNotNull { Mood.fromId(it) },
        focusTags = focusTags.mapNotNull { FocusArea.fromId(it) },
        keywordTags = keywordTags,
        rarity = Rarity.fromId(rarity),
        createdAt = createdAt
    )
}

fun SeedFortuneDto.toEntity(): FortuneEntity {
    return FortuneEntity(
        id = id,
        type = type,
        text = text,
        dailyIntention = dailyIntention,
        moodTags = moodTags,
        focusTags = focusTags,
        keywordTags = keywordTags,
        rarity = rarity,
        createdAt = createdAt,
        isSeen = false
    )
}
```

### 📄 6. `core/data/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/data/seed/SeedDataLoader.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.data.seed

import android.content.Context
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.mapper.toEntity
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.dao.FortuneDao
import com.google.gson.Gson
import com.google.gson.annotations.SerializedName
import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import timber.log.Timber
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

@Singleton
class SeedDataLoader @Inject constructor(
    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
    private val fortuneDao: FortuneDao
) {
    private val gson = Gson()

    suspend fun loadSeedDataIfNeeded() {
        val count = fortuneDao.count()
        if (count == 0) {
            loadSeedData()
        } else {
            Timber.d("Seed data already loaded: $count fortunes")
        }
    }

    private suspend fun loadSeedData() = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val jsonString = context.assets.open("seed_fortunes.json")
                .bufferedReader()
                .use { it.readText() }

            val seedData: SeedDataWrapper = gson.fromJson(jsonString, SeedDataWrapper::class.java)
            val entities = seedData.fortunes.map { it.toEntity() }
            fortuneDao.insertAll(entities)
            
            Timber.d("✅ Loaded ${entities.size} seed fortunes")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Timber.e(e, "❌ Seed data load failed")
        }
    }
}

data class SeedDataWrapper(
    @SerializedName("fortunes") val fortunes: List<SeedFortuneDto>
)

data class SeedFortuneDto(
    @SerializedName("id") val id: String,
    @SerializedName("type") val type: String,
    @SerializedName("text") val text: String,
    @SerializedName("dailyIntention") val dailyIntention: String,
    @SerializedName("moodTags") val moodTags: List<String>,
    @SerializedName("focusTags") val focusTags: List<String>,
    @SerializedName("keywordTags") val keywordTags: List<String>,
    @SerializedName("rarity") val rarity: String,
    @SerializedName("createdAt") val createdAt: Long
)
```

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository

import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.FocusArea
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.Fortune

interface FortuneRepository {
    suspend fun getFortune(
        type: FortuneType,
        mood: Mood,
        focus: FocusArea,
        seenIds: Set<String>
    ): Result<Fortune>

    suspend fun syncFromFirestore(): Result<Unit>
```

### 📄 8. `core/data/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/data/repository/FortuneRepositoryImpl.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository

import com.cynora.luna.core.data.mapper.toDomain
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.mapper.toEntity
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.FocusArea
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.Fortune
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.FortuneType
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.Mood
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.remote.FortuneDto
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.dao.FortuneDao
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.datastore.UserPreferencesDataStore
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.tasks.await
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import timber.log.Timber
import javax.inject.Inject

class FortuneRepositoryImpl @Inject constructor(
    private val fortuneDao: FortuneDao,
    private val firestore: FirebaseFirestore,
    private val smartPoolSelector: SmartPoolSelector,
    private val dataStore: UserPreferencesDataStore
) : FortuneRepository {

    override suspend fun getFortune(
        type: FortuneType,
        mood: Mood,
        focus: FocusArea,
        seenIds: Set<String>
    ): Result<Fortune> {
        return try {
            val localFortunes = fortuneDao.getAll().first().map { it.toDomain() }

            val selectedFortune = smartPoolSelector.selectFortune(
                pool = localFortunes,
                seenIds = seenIds,
                userMood = mood,
                userFocus = focus,
                fortuneType = type
            )

            if (selectedFortune != null) {
                Result.success(selectedFortune)
            } else {
                val syncResult = syncFromFirestore()
                
                if (syncResult.isFailure) {
                    Result.failure(Exception("Luna is working offline. Connect to sync new cosmic messages."))
                } else {
                    val refreshedFortunes = fortuneDao.getAll().first().map { it.toDomain() }
                    val retryFortune = smartPoolSelector.selectFortune(
                        pool = refreshedFortunes,
                        seenIds = seenIds,
                        userMood = mood,
                        userFocus = focus,
                        fortuneType = type
                    )
                    
                    if (retryFortune != null) Result.success(retryFortune)
                    else Result.failure(Exception("No fortune found right now. Please try again later."))
                }
            }
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Timber.e(e, "getFortune failed")
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    override suspend fun syncFromFirestore(): Result<Unit> {
        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val snapshot = firestore.collection("fortunes").get().await()
                val fortunes = snapshot.documents.mapNotNull { doc ->
                    doc.toObject(FortuneDto::class.java)?.toDomain()
                }
                fortuneDao.insertAll(fortunes.map { it.toEntity() })
                Timber.d("✅ Synced ${fortunes.size} fortunes from Firestore")
                Result.success(Unit)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Timber.e(e, "❌ Firestore sync failed")
                Result.failure(e)
            }
        }
    }

    override fun observeSeenIds(): Flow<Set<String>> {
        return dataStore.getSeenFortuneIds()
    }

    override suspend fun markAsSeen(fortuneId: String) {
        fortuneDao.markSeen(fortuneId)
        dataStore.updateSeenFortuneIds(fortuneId)
    }
}
```

---

### 📄 9. `core/data/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/data/repository/SmartPoolSelector.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository

import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.FocusArea
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.Fortune
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.FortuneType
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.Mood
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton
import kotlin.random.Random

@Singleton
class SmartPoolSelector @Inject constructor(
    private val random: Random = Random.Default
) {

    fun selectFortune(
        pool: List<Fortune>,
        seenIds: Set<String>,
        userMood: Mood,
        userFocus: FocusArea,
        fortuneType: FortuneType
    ): Fortune? {
        val candidates = pool.filter { fortune ->
            fortune.type == fortuneType && !seenIds.contains(fortune.id)
        }

        val availablePool = if (candidates.isEmpty()) {
            pool.filter { it.type == fortuneType }
        } else {
            candidates
        }

        if (availablePool.isEmpty()) return null

        val scoredFortunes = availablePool.map { fortune ->
            fortune to fortune.calculateMatchScore(userMood, userFocus)
        }

        val maxScore = scoredFortunes.maxOfOrNull { it.second } ?: 0
        val topCandidates = scoredFortunes
            .filter { it.second >= maxScore * 0.8 }
            .map { it.first }

        return if (topCandidates.isNotEmpty()) {
            topCandidates[random.nextInt(topCandidates.size)]
        } else {
            availablePool[random.nextInt(availablePool.size)]
        }
    }
}
```

---

### 📄 10. `core/data/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/data/repository/MysticCalendarRepository.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository

import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOf
import kotlinx.datetime.Clock
import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
import kotlinx.datetime.TimeZone
import kotlinx.datetime.toLocalDateTime
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

data class MysticCalendarData(
    val moonPhase: String,
    val planetInfo: String,
    val dailyEnergy: String,
    val luckyHour: String
)

@Singleton
class MysticCalendarRepository @Inject constructor() {
    
    fun observeDailyData(): Flow<MysticCalendarData> {
        val today = Clock.System.now().toLocalDateTime(TimeZone.currentSystemDefault()).date
        
        return flowOf(
            MysticCalendarData(
                moonPhase = calculateMoonPhase(today),
                planetInfo = getDailyPlanetInfo(today),
                dailyEnergy = getDailyEnergy(today),
                luckyHour = getLuckyHour(today)
            )
        )
    }
    
    private fun calculateMoonPhase(date: LocalDate): String {
        val knownNewMoon = LocalDate(2000, 1, 6)
        val daysSinceNewMoon = date.toEpochDays() - knownNewMoon.toEpochDays()
        val cyclePosition = (daysSinceNewMoon % 30).toInt()
        
        return when {
            cyclePosition < 2 -> "🌑 New Moon"
            cyclePosition < 7 -> "🌒 Waxing Crescent"
            cyclePosition < 10 -> "🌓 First Quarter"
            cyclePosition < 14 -> "🌔 Waxing Gibbous"
            cyclePosition < 16 -> "🌕 Full Moon"
            cyclePosition < 20 -> "🌖 Waning Gibbous"
            cyclePosition < 23 -> "🌗 Last Quarter"
            cyclePosition < 27 -> "🌘 Waning Crescent"
            else -> "🌑 New Moon"
        }
    }
    
    private fun getDailyPlanetInfo(date: LocalDate): String {
        val planets = listOf(
            "☿ Mercury Direct",
            "♀ Venus in Aries",
            "♂ Mars in Taurus",
            "♃ Jupiter in Gemini",
            "♄ Saturn in Pisces",
            "♅ Uranus in Taurus",
            "♆ Neptune in Pisces",
            "♇ Pluto in Aquarius"
        )
        return planets[date.dayOfYear % planets.size]
    }
    
    private fun getDailyEnergy(date: LocalDate): String {
        val energies = listOf(
            "Transformation & Renewal",
            "Creativity & Expression",
            "Communication & Connection",
            "Discipline & Focus",
            "Abundance & Prosperity",
            "Intuition & Insight",
            "Courage & Action"
        )
        // ✅ dayOfWeek.isoDayNumber (1-7, Pazartesi=1)
        return energies[(date.dayOfWeek.isoDayNumber - 1) % energies.size]
    }
    
    private fun getLuckyHour(date: LocalDate): String {
        val hours = listOf("06:00", "09:00", "12:00", "15:00", "18:00", "21:00")
        return hours[date.dayOfMonth % hours.size]
    }
}
```

### 📄 11. `core/data/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/data/repository/SocialRepository.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository

import com.cynora.luna.core.database.datastore.UserPreferencesDataStore
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import com.google.firebase.firestore.ListenerRegistration
import kotlinx.coroutines.channels.awaitClose
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.callbackFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.tasks.await
import timber.log.Timber
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

data class IntentionPost(
    val id: String = "",
    val mysticName: String = "",
    val avatarEmoji: String = "",
    val intentionText: String = "",
    val timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val likeCount: Int = 0,
    val likedBy: List<String> = emptyList()
)

@Singleton
class SocialRepository @Inject constructor(
    private val firestore: FirebaseFirestore,
    private val dataStore: UserPreferencesDataStore
) {
    private val postsCollection = firestore.collection("posts")
    
    // ✅ Thread-safe atomic reference
    private val userIdRef = AtomicReference<String?>(null)
    
    suspend fun ensureUserId() {
        if (userIdRef.get() == null) {
            userIdRef.set(dataStore.getOrCreateUserId())
        }
    }
    
    // ✅ Compose'dan çağrılabilir (suspend değil)
    fun currentUserIdSync(): String = userIdRef.get() ?: "anonymous"
    
    fun observePosts(): Flow<List<IntentionPost>> = callbackFlow {
        val registration: ListenerRegistration = postsCollection
            .orderBy("timestamp", com.google.firebase.firestore.Query.Direction.DESCENDING)
            .limit(50)
            .addSnapshotListener { snapshot, error ->
                if (error != null) {
                    Timber.e(error, "Firestore listen failed")
                    close(error)
                    return@addSnapshotListener
                }
                
                val posts = snapshot?.documents?.mapNotNull { doc ->
                    try {
                        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                        val likedBy = doc.get("likedBy") as? List<String> ?: emptyList()
                        
                        IntentionPost(
                            id = doc.id,
                            mysticName = doc.getString("mysticName") ?: "",
                            avatarEmoji = doc.getString("avatarEmoji") ?: "",
                            intentionText = doc.getString("intentionText") ?: "",
                            timestamp = doc.getLong("timestamp") ?: 0L,
                            likeCount = doc.getLong("likeCount")?.toInt() ?: 0,
                            likedBy = likedBy
                        )
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        Timber.e(e, "Post parse failed")
                        null
                    }
                } ?: emptyList()
                
                trySend(posts)
            }
        
        awaitClose { registration.remove() }
    }
    
    suspend fun addPost(post: IntentionPost): Result<Unit> {
        return try {
            val postData = mapOf(
                "mysticName" to post.mysticName,
                "avatarEmoji" to post.avatarEmoji,
                "intentionText" to post.intentionText,
                "timestamp" to post.timestamp,
                "likeCount" to 0,
                "likedBy" to emptyList<String>()
            )
            postsCollection.add(postData).await()
            Result.success(Unit)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Timber.e(e, "Add post failed")
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }
    
    suspend fun toggleLike(postId: String): Result<Boolean> {
        ensureUserId()
        val uid = userIdRef.get() ?: return Result.failure(IllegalStateException("User ID not initialized"))
        
        return try {
            val postRef = postsCollection.document(postId)
            
            val newIsLiked = firestore.runTransaction { transaction ->
                val snapshot = transaction.get(postRef)
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                val likedBy = (snapshot.get("likedBy") as? List<String>) ?: emptyList()
                val currentLikes = snapshot.getLong("likeCount") ?: 0L
                
                val isLiked = uid in likedBy
                val newLikedBy = if (isLiked) {
                    likedBy - uid
                } else {
                    likedBy + uid
                }
                val newLikeCount = if (isLiked) currentLikes - 1 else currentLikes + 1
                
                transaction.update(postRef, "likedBy", newLikedBy)
                transaction.update(postRef, "likeCount", newLikeCount)
                
                !isLiked
            }.await()
            
            Result.success(newIsLiked)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Timber.e(e, "Toggle like failed")
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }
}
```

### 📄 12. `core/data/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/data/repository/BirthChartRepository.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository

import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.ZodiacSign
import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
import kotlinx.datetime.LocalTime
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

data class BirthChartData(
    val sunSign: ZodiacSign,
    val moonSign: ZodiacSign,
    val risingSign: ZodiacSign,
    val mercurySign: ZodiacSign,
    val venusSign: ZodiacSign,
    val marsSign: ZodiacSign,
    val numerologyLifePath: Int,
    val chineseZodiac: String,
    val birthCard: String,
    val personality: String,
    val strengths: List<String>,
    val challenges: List<String>
)

data class BirthInfo(
    val birthDate: LocalDate,
    val birthTime: LocalTime?,
    val birthPlace: String?
)

@Singleton
class BirthChartRepository @Inject constructor() {
    
    fun calculateBirthChart(birthInfo: BirthInfo): BirthChartData {
        val sunSign = calculateSunSign(birthInfo.birthDate)
        val moonSign = calculateMoonSign(birthInfo.birthDate)
        val risingSign = calculateRisingSign(birthInfo.birthDate, birthInfo.birthTime)
        val mercurySign = calculateMercurySign(birthInfo.birthDate)
        val venusSign = calculateVenusSign(birthInfo.birthDate)
        val marsSign = calculateMarsSign(birthInfo.birthDate)
        val lifePath = calculateLifePathNumber(birthInfo.birthDate)
        val chineseZodiac = calculateChineseZodiac(birthInfo.birthDate.year)
        val birthCard = calculateBirthCard(birthInfo.birthDate)
        
        return BirthChartData(
            sunSign = sunSign,
            moonSign = moonSign,
            risingSign = risingSign,
            mercurySign = mercurySign,
            venusSign = venusSign,
            marsSign = marsSign,
            numerologyLifePath = lifePath,
            chineseZodiac = chineseZodiac,
            birthCard = birthCard,
            personality = getPersonality(sunSign, moonSign, risingSign),
            strengths = getStrengths(sunSign),
            challenges = getChallenges(sunSign)
        )
    }
    
    private fun calculateSunSign(date: LocalDate): ZodiacSign {
        val month = date.monthNumber
        val day = date.dayOfMonth
        return when {
            (month == 3 && day >= 21) || (month == 4 && day <= 19) -> ZodiacSign.ARIES
            (month == 4 && day >= 20) || (month == 5 && day <= 20) -> ZodiacSign.TAURUS
            (month == 5 && day >= 21) || (month == 6 && day <= 20) -> ZodiacSign.GEMINI
            (month == 6 && day >= 21) || (month == 7 && day <= 22) -> ZodiacSign.CANCER
            (month == 7 && day >= 23) || (month == 8 && day <= 22) -> ZodiacSign.LEO
            (month == 8 && day >= 23) || (month == 9 && day <= 22) -> ZodiacSign.VIRGO
            (month == 9 && day >= 23) || (month == 10 && day <= 22) -> ZodiacSign.LIBRA
            (month == 10 && day >= 23) || (month == 11 && day <= 21) -> ZodiacSign.SCORPIO
            (month == 11 && day >= 22) || (month == 12 && day <= 21) -> ZodiacSign.SAGITTARIUS
            (month == 12 && day >= 22) || (month == 1 && day <= 19) -> ZodiacSign.CAPRICORN
            (month == 1 && day >= 20) || (month == 2 && day <= 18) -> ZodiacSign.AQUARIUS
            else -> ZodiacSign.PISCES
        }
    }
    
    private fun calculateMoonSign(date: LocalDate): ZodiacSign {
        val knownMoonInAries = LocalDate(2000, 1, 6)
        val daysSince = date.toEpochDays() - knownMoonInAries.toEpochDays()
        val daysPerSign = 27.32 / 12.0
        val signIndex = ((daysSince / daysPerSign) % 12).toInt()
        return ZodiacSign.entries[(signIndex + 12) % 12]
    }
    
    private fun calculateRisingSign(date: LocalDate, time: LocalTime?): ZodiacSign {
        if (time == null) return calculateSunSign(date)
        val hour = time.hour
        val sunSign = calculateSunSign(date)
        val offset = hour / 2
        val risingIndex = (sunSign.ordinal + offset) % 12
        return ZodiacSign.entries[risingIndex]
    }
    
    private fun calculateMercurySign(date: LocalDate): ZodiacSign {
        val sunSign = calculateSunSign(date)
        val dayOfYear = date.dayOfYear
        val offset = if (dayOfYear % 2 == 0) 1 else -1
        val index = (sunSign.ordinal + offset + 12) % 12
        return ZodiacSign.entries[index]
    }
    
    private fun calculateVenusSign(date: LocalDate): ZodiacSign {
        val dayOfYear = date.dayOfYear
        val index = (dayOfYear / 15) % 12
        return ZodiacSign.entries[index]
    }
    
    private fun calculateMarsSign(date: LocalDate): ZodiacSign {
        val dayOfYear = date.dayOfYear
        val index = (dayOfYear / 20) % 12
        return ZodiacSign.entries[index]
    }
    
    private fun calculateLifePathNumber(date: LocalDate): Int {
        val allDigits = buildString {
            append(date.year)
            append(date.monthNumber)
            append(date.dayOfMonth)
        }.map { it.toString().toInt() }
        
        var sum = allDigits.sum()
        
        fun reduce(n: Int): Int {
            if (n == 11 || n == 22 || n == 33) return n
            if (n <= 9) return n
            return reduce(n.toString().map { it.toString().toInt() }.sum())
        }
        
        return reduce(sum)
    }
    
    private fun calculateChineseZodiac(year: Int): String {
        val animals = listOf("Rat 🐀", "Ox 🐂", "Tiger 🐅", "Rabbit 🐇", 
                           "Dragon 🐉", "Snake 🐍", "Horse 🐎", "Goat 🐐",
                           "Monkey 🐒", "Rooster 🐓", "Dog 🐕", "Pig 🐖")
        return animals[(year - 4) % 12]
    }
    
    private fun calculateBirthCard(date: LocalDate): String {
        val sum = date.dayOfMonth + date.monthNumber
        val cardNumber = if (sum > 22) sum - 22 else sum
        val cards = listOf(
            "The Fool", "The Magician", "The High Priestess", "The Empress",
            "The Emperor", "The Hierophant", "The Lovers", "The Chariot",
            "Strength", "The Hermit", "Wheel of Fortune", "Justice",
            "The Hanged Man", "Death", "Temperance", "The Devil",
            "The Tower", "The Star", "The Moon", "The Sun",
            "Judgement", "The World"
        )
        return cards[(cardNumber - 1) % 22]
    }
    
    private fun getPersonality(sun: ZodiacSign, moon: ZodiacSign, rising: ZodiacSign): String {
        return "With ${sun.displayName} Sun, ${moon.displayName} Moon, and ${rising.displayName} Rising, " +
               "you have a ${getPersonalityTrait(sun)} soul. " +
               "Your emotional world is shaped by ${moon.displayName}'s depth, " +
               "while you appear to the world with ${rising.displayName}'s energy."
    }
    
    private fun getPersonalityTrait(sign: ZodiacSign): String {
        return when (sign) {
            ZodiacSign.ARIES -> "bold and pioneering"
            ZodiacSign.TAURUS -> "steady and reliable"
            ZodiacSign.GEMINI -> "curious and communicative"
            ZodiacSign.CANCER -> "nurturing and protective"
            ZodiacSign.LEO -> "charismatic and creative"
            ZodiacSign.VIRGO -> "analytical and perfectionist"
            ZodiacSign.LIBRA -> "diplomatic and harmonious"
            ZodiacSign.SCORPIO -> "passionate and deep"
            ZodiacSign.SAGITTARIUS -> "adventurous and free-spirited"
            ZodiacSign.CAPRICORN -> "disciplined and ambitious"
            ZodiacSign.AQUARIUS -> "innovative and visionary"
            ZodiacSign.PISCES -> "intuitive and empathetic"
        }
    }
    
    private fun getStrengths(sign: ZodiacSign): List<String> {
        return when (sign) {
            ZodiacSign.ARIES -> listOf("Courage", "Leadership", "Energy", "Honesty")
            ZodiacSign.TAURUS -> listOf("Patience", "Reliability", "Practicality", "Loyalty")
            ZodiacSign.GEMINI -> listOf("Intelligence", "Adaptability", "Communication", "Curiosity")
            ZodiacSign.CANCER -> listOf("Empathy", "Compassion", "Intuition", "Protectiveness")
            ZodiacSign.LEO -> listOf("Charisma", "Creativity", "Generosity", "Confidence")
            ZodiacSign.VIRGO -> listOf("Analytical", "Attention to Detail", "Organization", "Service")
            ZodiacSign.LIBRA -> listOf("Diplomacy", "Justice", "Harmony", "Romance")
            ZodiacSign.SCORPIO -> listOf("Passion", "Determination", "Intuition", "Transformation")
            ZodiacSign.SAGITTARIUS -> listOf("Optimism", "Adventure", "Philosophy", "Honesty")
            ZodiacSign.CAPRICORN -> listOf("Discipline", "Ambition", "Responsibility", "Strategy")
            ZodiacSign.AQUARIUS -> listOf("Innovation", "Independence", "Humanity", "Vision")
            ZodiacSign.PISCES -> listOf("Intuition", "Empathy", "Creativity", "Spirituality")
        }
    }
    
    private fun getChallenges(sign: ZodiacSign): List<String> {
        return when (sign) {
            ZodiacSign.ARIES -> listOf("Impatience", "Impulsiveness", "Stubbornness")
            ZodiacSign.TAURUS -> listOf("Stubbornness", "Comfort Zone", "Jealousy")
            ZodiacSign.GEMINI -> listOf("Indecisiveness", "Superficiality", "Restlessness")
            ZodiacSign.CANCER -> listOf("Over-emotionality", "Holding onto past", "Moodiness")
            ZodiacSign.LEO -> listOf("Pride", "Need for attention", "Stubbornness")
            ZodiacSign.VIRGO -> listOf("Perfectionism", "Worry", "Critical nature")
            ZodiacSign.LIBRA -> listOf("Indecisiveness", "Conflict avoidance", "Dependency")
            ZodiacSign.SCORPIO -> listOf("Jealousy", "Revenge", "Trust issues")
            ZodiacSign.SAGITTARIUS -> listOf("Carelessness", "Bluntness", "Impatience")
            ZodiacSign.CAPRICORN -> listOf("Workaholism", "Rigidity", "Pessimism")
            ZodiacSign.AQUARIUS -> listOf("Emotional distance", "Rebelliousness", "Detachment")
            ZodiacSign.PISCES -> listOf("Escapism", "Oversensitivity", "Victim mentality")
        }
    }
}
```

### 📄 13. `core/data/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/data/repository/CompatibilityRepository.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository

import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.ZodiacSign
import javax.inject.Inject
import javax.inject.Singleton

data class CompatibilityResult(
    val overallScore: Int,
    val loveScore: Int,
    val friendshipScore: Int,
    val communicationScore: Int,
    val trustScore: Int,
    val passionScore: Int,
    val strengths: List<String>,
    val challenges: List<String>,
    val advice: String,
    val element: String
)

enum class Element(val displayName: String, val emoji: String) {
    FIRE("Fire", "🔥"),
    EARTH("Earth", "🌍"),
    AIR("Air", "💨"),
    WATER("Water", "💧")
}

@Singleton
class CompatibilityRepository @Inject constructor() {
    
    fun calculateCompatibility(sign1: ZodiacSign, sign2: ZodiacSign): CompatibilityResult {
        val element1 = getElement(sign1)
        val element2 = getElement(sign2)
        
        val elementScore = calculateElementCompatibility(element1, element2)
        val modalityScore = calculateModalityCompatibility(sign1, sign2)
        val polarityScore = calculatePolarityCompatibility(sign1, sign2)
        
        val overallScore = ((elementScore * 0.5) + (modalityScore * 0.3) + (polarityScore * 0.2)).toInt()
        
        return CompatibilityResult(
            overallScore = overallScore,
            loveScore = calculateLoveScore(element1, element2),
            friendshipScore = calculateFriendshipScore(element1, element2),
            communicationScore = calculateCommunicationScore(element1, element2),
            trustScore = calculateTrustScore(element1, element2),
            passionScore = calculatePassionScore(element1, element2),
            strengths = getStrengths(element1, element2),
            challenges = getChallenges(element1, element2),
            advice = getAdvice(element1, element2),
            element = "${element1.emoji} ${element1.displayName} + ${element2.emoji} ${element2.displayName}"
        )
    }
    
    private fun getElement(sign: ZodiacSign): Element {
        return when (sign) {
            ZodiacSign.ARIES, ZodiacSign.LEO, ZodiacSign.SAGITTARIUS -> Element.FIRE
            ZodiacSign.TAURUS, ZodiacSign.VIRGO, ZodiacSign.CAPRICORN -> Element.EARTH
            ZodiacSign.GEMINI, ZodiacSign.LIBRA, ZodiacSign.AQUARIUS -> Element.AIR
            ZodiacSign.CANCER, ZodiacSign.SCORPIO, ZodiacSign.PISCES -> Element.WATER
        }
    }
    
    private val elementScores = mapOf(
        "${Element.FIRE}+${Element.FIRE}" to 85,
        "${Element.FIRE}+${Element.AIR}" to 95,
        "${Element.FIRE}+${Element.EARTH}" to 60,
        "${Element.FIRE}+${Element.WATER}" to 55,
        "${Element.EARTH}+${Element.EARTH}" to 80,
        "${Element.EARTH}+${Element.WATER}" to 95,
        "${Element.EARTH}+${Element.AIR}" to 55,
        "${Element.AIR}+${Element.AIR}" to 85,
        "${Element.AIR}+${Element.WATER}" to 60,
        "${Element.WATER}+${Element.WATER}" to 80
    )
    
    private fun calculateElementCompatibility(elem1: Element, elem2: Element): Int {
        val key1 = "$elem1+$elem2"
        val key2 = "$elem2+$elem1"
        return elementScores[key1] ?: elementScores[key2] ?: 70
    }
    
    private fun calculateModalityCompatibility(sign1: ZodiacSign, sign2: ZodiacSign): Int {
        val modality1 = getModality(sign1)
        val modality2 = getModality(sign2)
        return if (modality1 == modality2) 70 else 85
    }
    
    private fun getModality(sign: ZodiacSign): String {
        return when (sign) {
            ZodiacSign.ARIES, ZodiacSign.CANCER, ZodiacSign.LIBRA, ZodiacSign.CAPRICORN -> "Cardinal"
            ZodiacSign.TAURUS, ZodiacSign.LEO, ZodiacSign.SCORPIO, ZodiacSign.AQUARIUS -> "Fixed"
            ZodiacSign.GEMINI, ZodiacSign.VIRGO, ZodiacSign.SAGITTARIUS, ZodiacSign.PISCES -> "Mutable"
        }
    }
    
    private fun calculatePolarityCompatibility(sign1: ZodiacSign, sign2: ZodiacSign): Int {
        val polarity1 = getPolarity(sign1)
        val polarity2 = getPolarity(sign2)
        return if (polarity1 != polarity2) 90 else 75
    }
    
    private fun getPolarity(sign: ZodiacSign): String {
        return when (sign) {
            ZodiacSign.ARIES, ZodiacSign.GEMINI, ZodiacSign.LEO, 
            ZodiacSign.LIBRA, ZodiacSign.SAGITTARIUS, ZodiacSign.AQUARIUS -> "Masculine"
            else -> "Feminine"
        }
    }
    
    private fun calculateLoveScore(elem1: Element, elem2: Element): Int {
        return (calculateElementCompatibility(elem1, elem2) * 1.05).toInt().coerceAtMost(100)
    }
    
    private fun calculateFriendshipScore(elem1: Element, elem2: Element): Int {
        return (calculateElementCompatibility(elem1, elem2) * 0.95).toInt()
    }
    
    private fun calculateCommunicationScore(elem1: Element, elem2: Element): Int {
        return when {
            elem1 == elem2 -> 85
            (elem1 == Element.AIR && elem2 == Element.FIRE) || 
            (elem2 == Element.AIR && elem1 == Element.FIRE) -> 95
            else -> 70
        }
    }
    
    private fun calculateTrustScore(elem1: Element, elem2: Element): Int {
        return (calculateElementCompatibility(elem1, elem2) * 0.9).toInt()
    }
    
    private fun calculatePassionScore(elem1: Element, elem2: Element): Int {
        return when {
            elem1 == Element.FIRE && elem2 == Element.FIRE -> 95
            (elem1 == Element.FIRE && elem2 == Element.AIR) || 
            (elem2 == Element.FIRE && elem1 == Element.AIR) -> 90
            elem1 == Element.WATER && elem2 == Element.WATER -> 85
            else -> 70
        }
    }
    
    private fun getStrengths(elem1: Element, elem2: Element): List<String> {
        return when {
            elem1 == elem2 -> listOf("Deep understanding", "Shared values", "Natural harmony")
            (elem1 == Element.FIRE && elem2 == Element.AIR) || 
            (elem2 == Element.FIRE && elem1 == Element.AIR) -> 
                listOf("Inspiring communication", "Shared passion", "Dynamic energy")
            (elem1 == Element.EARTH && elem2 == Element.WATER) || 
            (elem2 == Element.EARTH && elem1 == Element.WATER) -> 
                listOf("Emotional security", "Practical support", "Deep bond")
            (elem1 == Element.FIRE && elem2 == Element.FIRE) -> 
                listOf("Shared passion", "Mutual understanding", "Dynamic energy")
            (elem1 == Element.EARTH && elem2 == Element.EARTH) -> 
                listOf("Stability", "Reliability", "Practical support")
            (elem1 == Element.AIR && elem2 == Element.AIR) -> 
                listOf("Intellectual connection", "Great communication", "Shared ideas")
            (elem1 == Element.WATER && elem2 == Element.WATER) -> 
                listOf("Emotional depth", "Intuitive bond", "Compassion")
            else -> listOf("Complementary traits", "Growth opportunity", "New perspectives")
        }
    }
    
    private fun getChallenges(elem1: Element, elem2: Element): List<String> {
        return when {
            (elem1 == Element.FIRE && elem2 == Element.WATER) || 
            (elem2 == Element.FIRE && elem1 == Element.WATER) -> 
                listOf("Emotional conflict", "Different needs", "Requires patience")
            (elem1 == Element.AIR && elem2 == Element.EARTH) || 
            (elem2 == Element.AIR && elem1 == Element.EARTH) -> 
                listOf("Communication gaps", "Different priorities", "Needs understanding")
            else -> listOf("Areas requiring compromise", "Different approaches")
        }
    }
    
    private fun getAdvice(elem1: Element, elem2: Element): String {
        return when {
            elem1 == elem2 -> 
                "You share the power of the same element. You understand each other deeply but may share the same blind spots. Stay open to different perspectives."
            (elem1 == Element.FIRE && elem2 == Element.AIR) || 
            (elem2 == Element.FIRE && elem1 == Element.AIR) -> 
                "Air feeds fire! Your communication is strong, your energy high. You can achieve great things together. Just remember to be patient."
            (elem1 == Element.EARTH && elem2 == Element.WATER) || 
            (elem2 == Element.EARTH && elem1 == Element.WATER) -> 
                "Water nourishes earth. Emotional depth and practical security in perfect harmony. Embrace each other's differences."
            else -> 
                "Different elements can be challenging but rewarding. You have much to learn from each other. Patience and open communication are your keys."
        }
    }
}
```

## 📄 14. `core/data/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/core/data/repository/AngelNumbersRepository.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository

import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOf

            spiritualMessage = "In perfect harmony with the universe. Set intention.",
            action = "Make a wish at 11:11 and visualize."
        ),
        "1212" to AngelNumber(
            number = "1212",
            meaning = "Completion and new cycle. Time of ascension.",
            loveMessage = "New level in relationship.",
            careerMessage = "Completion and new beginning in career.",
            spiritualMessage = "Spiritual ascension to the 12th dimension.",
            action = "Close an old cycle today, set a new intention."
        ),
        "1234" to AngelNumber(
            number = "1234",
            meaning = "Step by step progress. Simple and clear path.",
            loveMessage = "Progress step by step in relationship.",
            careerMessage = "Progress in career with simple steps.",
            spiritualMessage = "Spiritual journey is simple and clear.",
            action = "Take a small but clear step today."

        "2222" to AngelNumber(
            number = "2222",
            meaning = "Master builder. Big dreams are becoming reality.",
            loveMessage = "Work for your dream relationship.",
            careerMessage = "Master plan for big projects.",
            spiritualMessage = "Master level spiritually.",
            action = "Write down a big dream today and plan it."
        )
    )
    
    fun getAngelNumber(number: String): AngelNumber? {
        return angelNumbers[number]
    }
    
    fun getAllAngelNumbers(): Flow<List<AngelNumber>> {
        return flowOf(angelNumbers.values.toList())
    }
    
    fun getRandomAngelNumber(): AngelNumber {
        return angelNumbers.values.toList().random()
    }
    
    fun searchAngelNumber(query: String): List<AngelNumber> {
        return angelNumbers.values.filter { 
            it.number.contains(query) || 
            it.meaning.contains(query, ignoreCase = true) 
        }
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository

    )
    
    fun getDailyAffirmation(): Affirmation {
        val today = Clock.System.now().toLocalDateTime(TimeZone.currentSystemDefault()).date
        val dayOfYear = today.dayOfYear
        return affirmations[dayOfYear % affirmations.size]
    }
    
    fun getAffirmationsByCategory(category: AffirmationCategory): List<Affirmation> {
        return affirmations.filter { it.category == category }
    }
    
    fun getAllAffirmations(): Flow<List<Affirmation>> {
        return flowOf(affirmations)
    }
    
    fun getRandomAffirmation(): Affirmation {
        return affirmations.random()
    }
}
```
