### 📄 2. `feature/fortune/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/feature/fortune/FortuneViewModel.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune

import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.FocusArea
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.Fortune
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.FortuneType
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.Mood
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.FortuneRepository
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.datastore.UserPreferencesDataStore
import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.channels.BufferOverflow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableSharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asSharedFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.datetime.Clock
import kotlinx.datetime.TimeZone
import kotlinx.datetime.toLocalDateTime
import javax.inject.Inject

@Immutable
data class FortuneState(
    val isLoading: Boolean = false,
    val fortune: Fortune? = null,
    val selectedType: FortuneType = FortuneType.COFFEE,
    val selectedMood: Mood = Mood.HOPEFUL,
    val selectedFocus: FocusArea = FocusArea.GENERAL,
    val error: String? = null,
    val isTypewriterComplete: Boolean = false
)

sealed interface FortuneIntent {
    data class SelectType(val type: FortuneType) : FortuneIntent
    data class SelectMood(val mood: Mood) : FortuneIntent
    data class SelectFocus(val focus: FocusArea) : FortuneIntent
    data object GetFortune : FortuneIntent
    data object Retry : FortuneIntent
    data object MarkAsSeen : FortuneIntent
    data object OnTypewriterComplete : FortuneIntent
}

sealed interface FortuneEffect {
    data class ShowSnackbar(val message: String) : FortuneEffect
}

@HiltViewModel
class FortuneViewModel @Inject constructor(
    private val repository: FortuneRepository,
    private val dataStore: UserPreferencesDataStore
) : ViewModel() {

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(FortuneState())
    val state: StateFlow<FortuneState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    // ✅ Buffer eklendi - effect kaybı önlendi
    private val _effects = MutableSharedFlow<FortuneEffect>(
        extraBufferCapacity = 10,
        onBufferOverflow = BufferOverflow.DROP_OLDEST
    )
    val effects: SharedFlow<FortuneEffect> = _effects.asSharedFlow()

    private val _seenIds = MutableStateFlow<Set<String>>(emptySet())

    init {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.observeSeenIds().collect { ids ->
                _seenIds.value = ids
            }
        }
    }

    fun onIntent(intent: FortuneIntent) {
        when (intent) {
            is FortuneIntent.SelectType -> _state.update { it.copy(selectedType = intent.type) }
            is FortuneIntent.SelectMood -> _state.update { it.copy(selectedMood = intent.mood) }
            is FortuneIntent.SelectFocus -> _state.update { it.copy(selectedFocus = intent.focus) }
            is FortuneIntent.GetFortune -> getFortune()
            is FortuneIntent.Retry -> getFortune()
            is FortuneIntent.MarkAsSeen -> markAsSeen()
            is FortuneIntent.OnTypewriterComplete -> {
                _state.update { it.copy(isTypewriterComplete = true) }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun getFortune() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _state.update { it.copy(isLoading = true, error = null, isTypewriterComplete = false) }

            val result = repository.getFortune(
                type = _state.value.selectedType,
                mood = _state.value.selectedMood,
                focus = _state.value.selectedFocus,
                seenIds = _seenIds.value
            )

            result.fold(
                onSuccess = { fortune ->
                    _state.update { it.copy(isLoading = false, fortune = fortune, error = null) }
                    dataStore.incrementFortuneCount()
                },
                onFailure = { error ->
                    _state.update { it.copy(isLoading = false, error = error.message) }
                    // ✅ tryEmit kullanıldı
                    _effects.tryEmit(FortuneEffect.ShowSnackbar(error.message ?: "Fortune failed"))
                }
            )
        }
    }

    private fun markAsSeen() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val fortuneId = _state.value.fortune?.id ?: return@launch
            repository.markAsSeen(fortuneId)
            _seenIds.update { it + fortuneId }
            
            // ✅ UTC ile streak güncelle - timezone bağımsız
            val todayUtc = Clock.System.now()
                .toLocalDateTime(TimeZone.UTC).date.toString()
            dataStore.updateStreak(todayUtc)
        }
    }
}
```

```kotlin

import kotlinx.coroutines.delay

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun FortuneScreen(
    initialFortuneId: String? = null,
    viewModel: FortuneViewModel = hiltViewModel()
) {
    val state by viewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val snackbarHostState = remember { SnackbarHostState() }

    // Deep link handling
    LaunchedEffect(initialFortuneId) {
        if (!initialFortuneId.isNullOrEmpty()) {
            // TODO: ViewModel'e loadFortuneById metodu eklenebilir
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(viewModel) {
        viewModel.effects.collect { effect ->
            when (effect) {
                is FortuneEffect.ShowSnackbar -> {
                    snackbarHostState.showSnackbar(
                        message = effect.message,
                        duration = SnackbarDuration.Short
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    Scaffold(
        snackbarHost = {
            SnackbarHost(snackbarHostState) { data ->
                Snackbar(
                    snackbarData = data,
                    containerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark,
                    contentColor = Color.White,
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
                )
            }
        }
    ) { paddingValues ->
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(paddingValues)
                .background(LunaColors.DeepBlack)
        ) {
            if (state.fortune == null) {
                FortuneSelectionContent(state, viewModel::onIntent)
            } else {
                FortuneResultContent(state, viewModel::onIntent)
            }

            if (state.isLoading) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.7f)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(color = LunaColors.Gold)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                        Text(
                            "Reading the cosmos...",
                            color = LunaColors.Gold,
                            fontSize = 16.sp
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun FortuneSelectionContent(

) {
    Column(

            .fillMaxSize()

            text = "What do you seek today?",

            color = Color.White,
            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
        )

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

        Text(
            text = "Choose your mood and focus, receive the universe's message",
            fontSize = 16.sp,
            color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.6f),
            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
        )

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(32.dp))

        Text("Fortune Type", fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = LunaColors.Gold)
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

        androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.grid.LazyVerticalGrid(
            columns = androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.grid.GridCells.Fixed(2),

}

@Composable
private fun FortuneResultContent(
    state: FortuneState,
    onIntent: (FortuneIntent) -> Unit
) {
    val fortune = state.fortune ?: return
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val fullText = fortune.text

    // ✅ fortune.id ile key - yeni fortune gelince sıfırlansın
    var targetLength by remember(fortune.id) { mutableIntStateOf(0) }
    var reported by remember(fortune.id) { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val animatedLength by animateIntAsState(
        targetValue = targetLength,
        animationSpec = tween(
            durationMillis = (fullText.length * 25).coerceIn(400, 2500),
            easing = LinearEasing
        ),
        label = "typewriter"
    )

    // Animasyonu başlat
    LaunchedEffect(fortune.id) {
        targetLength = 0
        reported = false
        delay(150)
        targetLength = fullText.length
    }

    // ✅ KRİTİK: Bitiş tespiti + seen işaretleme
    LaunchedEffect(animatedLength, fullText.length) {
        if (!reported && animatedLength >= fullText.length && fullText.isNotEmpty()) {
            reported = true
            onIntent(FortuneIntent.MarkAsSeen)
            onIntent(FortuneIntent.OnTypewriterComplete)
        }
    }

    val visibleText = fullText.take(animatedLength)

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
            .padding(24.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(32.dp))

        Text(
            text = fortune.type.icon + " " + fortune.type.displayName,
            fontSize = 14.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
            color = LunaColors.Gold
        )

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))

        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                .background(LunaColors.SurfaceDark.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
                .border(
                    width = 1.dp,
                    brush = Brush.linearGradient(
                        colors = listOf(
                            Color.Transparent,
                            LunaColors.Gold.copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                            Color.Transparent
                        )
                    ),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
                )
                .padding(24.dp)
        ) {
            Column {
                Text(
                    text = visibleText,
                    fontSize = 18.sp,
                    lineHeight = 28.sp,
                    color = Color.White,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                )

                if (!state.isTypewriterComplete) {
                    Text(text = "▌", color = LunaColors.Gold, fontSize = 18.sp)
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(32.dp))

        AnimatedVisibility(
            visible = state.isTypewriterComplete,
            enter = fadeIn() + slideInVertically { it }
        ) {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                    .background(LunaColors.SurfaceVariant)
                    .padding(20.dp),
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "✨ Daily Intention",
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                    color = LunaColors.Gold
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                Text(
                    text = fortune.dailyIntention,
                    fontSize = 16.sp,
                    color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.9f),
                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                )
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))

        AnimatedVisibility(
            visible = state.isTypewriterComplete,
            enter = fadeIn()
        ) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
            ) {
                OutlinedButton(
                    onClick = { onIntent(FortuneIntent.GetFortune) },
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).height(48.dp),
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.outlinedButtonColors(contentColor = LunaColors.Gold)
                ) {
                    Text("Try Again")
                }

                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        ShareUtils.shareFortune(
                            context = context,
                            fortuneText = fortune.text,
                            dailyIntention = fortune.dailyIntention,
                            fortuneType = fortune.type.displayName
                        )
                    },
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f).height(48.dp),
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = LunaColors.Gold)
                ) {
                    Text("Share", color = Color(0xFF121212))
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))
    }
}

@Composable
private fun FortuneTypeCard(type: FortuneType, isSelected: Boolean, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(80.dp)
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .then(
                if (isSelected) Modifier.border(2.dp, LunaColors.Gold, RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                else Modifier
            ),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
            containerColor = if (isSelected) LunaColors.Gold.copy(alpha = 0.1f) else LunaColors.SurfaceDark
        ),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
    ) {
        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                Text(text = type.icon, fontSize = 24.sp)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                Text(
                    text = type.displayName,
                    fontSize = 12.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                    color = if (isSelected) LunaColors.Gold else Color.White,
                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun MoodChip(mood: Mood, isSelected: Boolean, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    FilterChip(
        selected = isSelected,

        label = { Text(text = mood.displayName, fontSize = 12.sp) },
        colors = FilterChipDefaults.filterChipColors(
            selectedContainerColor = LunaColors.Gold.copy(alpha = 0.2f),
            selectedLabelColor = LunaColors.Gold
        )
    )
}

        selected = isSelected,
        onClick = onClick,
        label = { Text(text = focus.displayName, fontSize = 12.sp) },

            selectedContainerColor = LunaColors.Gold.copy(alpha = 0.2f),

        )
    )
}
```

### 📄 4. `feature/fortune/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/feature/fortune/birthchart/BirthChartViewModel.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.birthchart

import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.BirthChartRepository
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.BirthChartData
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.BirthInfo
import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.datetime.LocalDate
import kotlinx.datetime.LocalTime
import javax.inject.Inject

@Immutable
data class BirthChartState(
    val birthInfo: BirthInfo? = null,
    val birthChart: BirthChartData? = null,
    val isLoading: Boolean = false,
    val error: String? = null
)

sealed interface BirthChartIntent {
    data class SubmitBirthInfo(val date: LocalDate, val time: LocalTime?, val place: String) : BirthChartIntent
    data object Reset : BirthChartIntent
}

@HiltViewModel
class BirthChartViewModel @Inject constructor(
    private val repository: BirthChartRepository
) : ViewModel() {
    
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(BirthChartState())
    val state: StateFlow<BirthChartState> = _state.asStateFlow()
    
    fun onIntent(intent: BirthChartIntent) {
        when (intent) {
            is BirthChartIntent.SubmitBirthInfo -> {
                viewModelScope.launch {
                    _state.update { it.copy(isLoading = true) }
                    try {
                        val birthInfo = BirthInfo(intent.date, intent.time, intent.place)
                        val chart = repository.calculateBirthChart(birthInfo)
                        _state.update { 
                            it.copy(birthInfo = birthInfo, birthChart = chart, isLoading = false)
                        }
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        _state.update { it.copy(isLoading = false, error = e.message) }
                    }
                }
            }
            is BirthChartIntent.Reset -> _state.update { BirthChartState() }
        }
    }
}
```

```kotlin
@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun BirthChartScreen(
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    viewModel: BirthChartViewModel = hiltViewModel()
) {
    val state by viewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    
    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = { Text("Birth Chart 🌌") },

                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .background(LunaColors.DeepBlack)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())

        ) {
            if (state.birthInfo == null) {
                BirthInfoForm { date, time, place ->
                    viewModel.onIntent(BirthChartIntent.SubmitBirthInfo(date, time, place))
                }

                    onClick = { viewModel.onIntent(BirthChartIntent.Reset) },
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = LunaColors.Gold)
                ) {
                    Text("Calculate Another", color = Color.Black)
                }
            }
        }
    }

@Composable
private fun BirthChartResult(birthChart: BirthChartData) {
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
            .border(
                width = 1.dp,
                brush = Brush.linearGradient(listOf(LunaColors.Gold, LunaColors.MysticPurple, LunaColors.Gold)),

        ) {

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(20.dp))
            Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceEvenly) {
                BigThreeItem("☀️ Sun", birthChart.sunSign.symbol + " " + birthChart.sunSign.displayName)
                BigThreeItem("🌙 Moon", birthChart.moonSign.symbol + " " + birthChart.moonSign.displayName)
                BigThreeItem("⬆️ Rising", birthChart.risingSign.symbol + " " + birthChart.risingSign.displayName)
            }
        }
    }
    
    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
    
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(20.dp)) {

    }

@Composable

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
        Text(value, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color.White)
    }
}

@Composable

    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 4.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
    ) {
        Text(planet, fontSize = 14.sp, color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.8f))
        Text(sign, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = LunaColors.Gold)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun InfoRow(label: String, value: String) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 4.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
    ) {
        Text(label, fontSize = 14.sp, color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.8f))
        Text(value, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = LunaColors.Gold)
    }
}
```

### 📄 6. `feature/fortune/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/feature/fortune/dailycard/DailyCardViewModel.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.dailycard

import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.DailyCardRepository
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.TarotCard
import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import javax.inject.Inject

@Immutable
data class DailyCardState(
    val card: TarotCard? = null,
    val isRevealed: Boolean = false
)

sealed interface DailyCardIntent {
    data object RevealCard : DailyCardIntent
    data object DrawNewCard : DailyCardIntent
}

@HiltViewModel
class DailyCardViewModel @Inject constructor(
    private val repository: DailyCardRepository
) : ViewModel() {
    
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(DailyCardState())
    val state: StateFlow<DailyCardState> = _state.asStateFlow()
    
    fun onIntent(intent: DailyCardIntent) {
        when (intent) {
            is DailyCardIntent.RevealCard -> {
                _state.update { it.copy(card = repository.getDailyCard(), isRevealed = true) }
            }
            is DailyCardIntent.DrawNewCard -> {
                _state.update { it.copy(card = repository.getRandomCard(), isRevealed = true) }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```kotlin
import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import androidx.hilt.navigation.compose.hiltViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import com.cynora.luna.core.ui.theme.LunaColors
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun DailyCardScreen(
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    viewModel: DailyCardViewModel = hiltViewModel()
) {
    val state by viewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    var isFlipped by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    // ✅ Double-tap koruması
    var isAnimating by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    
    val rotation by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isFlipped) 180f else 0f,
        animationSpec = tween(800, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
        label = "flip"
    )
    
    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = { Text("Card of the Day 🎯") },
                navigationIcon = {
                    IconButton(onClick = onBack) {
                        Icon(Icons.Default.ArrowBack, "Back", tint = Color.White)
                    }
                },
                colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(
                    containerColor = LunaColors.DeepBlack,
                    titleContentColor = LunaColors.Gold
                )
            )
        }
    ) { padding ->
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .background(LunaColors.DeepBlack)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(16.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            if (!isFlipped) {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(280.dp)
                        .rotate(rotation)
                        .clickable(enabled = !isAnimating) {
                            if (!isFlipped) {
                                isAnimating = true
                                isFlipped = true
                                viewModel.onIntent(DailyCardIntent.RevealCard)
                                scope.launch {
                                    delay(800)
                                    isAnimating = false
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        .border(
                            width = 2.dp,
                            brush = Brush.linearGradient(listOf(LunaColors.Gold, LunaColors.MysticPurple)),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                        ),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                ) {
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                        Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                            Text("✨", fontSize = 64.sp)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                            Text(
                                "Tap to reveal\nyour card",
                                fontSize = 18.sp,
                                color = LunaColors.Gold,
                                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))
                Text("Take a deep breath and tap the card", fontSize = 14.sp, color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
            } else {
                state.card?.let { card ->
                    Card(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(280.dp)
                            .rotate(rotation)
                            .border(
                                width = 2.dp,
                                brush = Brush.linearGradient(listOf(LunaColors.Gold, LunaColors.MysticPurple)),
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                            ),
                        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                    ) {
                        Column(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(20.dp),
                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                        ) {
                            Text(card.emoji, fontSize = 72.sp)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                            Text(card.name, fontSize = 22.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = LunaColors.Gold)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                            Text(
                                card.keywords.joinToString(" • "),
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.7f),
                                textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))
                    
                    MeaningSection("✨ General", card.generalMeaning)
                    MeaningSection("💕 Love", card.loveMeaning)
                    MeaningSection("💼 Career", card.careerMeaning)
                    MeaningSection("🙏 Spiritual", card.spiritualMeaning)
                    
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                    
                    Card(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.Gold.copy(alpha = 0.1f)),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                    ) {
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                            Text("💡 Today's Advice", fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = LunaColors.Gold)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                            Text(card.advice, fontSize = 14.sp, color = Color.White)
                        }
                    }
                    
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                    
                    Card(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.MysticPurple.copy(alpha = 0.1f)),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
                    ) {
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                            Text("🌟 Affirmation", fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = LunaColors.MysticPurple)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                            Text(
                                "\"${card.affirmation}\"",
                                fontSize = 16.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                                color = Color.White,
                                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                    
                    Button(
                        onClick = {
                            isAnimating = true
                            isFlipped = false
                            viewModel.onIntent(DailyCardIntent.DrawNewCard)
                            scope.launch {
                                delay(800)
                                isAnimating = false
                            }
                        },
                        enabled = !isAnimating,
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = LunaColors.Gold)
                    ) {
                        Text("Draw Another Card", color = Color.Black)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun MeaningSection(title: String, meaning: String) {
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 4.dp),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
            Text(title, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = LunaColors.Gold)
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
            Text(meaning, fontSize = 14.sp, color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.9f), lineHeight = 20.sp)
        }
    }
}
```

### 📄 8. `feature/fortune/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/feature/fortune/compatibility/CompatibilityViewModel.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.compatibility

import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.ZodiacSign
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.CompatibilityRepository
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.CompatibilityResult
import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import javax.inject.Inject

@Immutable
data class CompatibilityState(
    val sign1: ZodiacSign? = null,
    val sign2: ZodiacSign? = null,
    val result: CompatibilityResult? = null
)

sealed interface CompatibilityIntent {
    data class SelectSign1(val sign: ZodiacSign) : CompatibilityIntent
    data class SelectSign2(val sign: ZodiacSign) : CompatibilityIntent
    data object Calculate : CompatibilityIntent
    data object Reset : CompatibilityIntent
}

@HiltViewModel
class CompatibilityViewModel @Inject constructor(
    private val repository: CompatibilityRepository
) : ViewModel() {
    
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(CompatibilityState())
    val state: StateFlow<CompatibilityState> = _state.asStateFlow()
    
    fun onIntent(intent: CompatibilityIntent) {
        when (intent) {
            is CompatibilityIntent.SelectSign1 -> _state.update { it.copy(sign1 = intent.sign) }
            is CompatibilityIntent.SelectSign2 -> _state.update { it.copy(sign2 = intent.sign) }
            is CompatibilityIntent.Calculate -> {
                val s1 = _state.value.sign1 ?: return
                val s2 = _state.value.sign2 ?: return
                val result = repository.calculateCompatibility(s1, s2)
                _state.update { it.copy(result = result) }
            }
            is CompatibilityIntent.Reset -> _state.update { CompatibilityState() }
        }
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.compatibility

import androidx.compose.foundation.border

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun CompatibilityScreen(
    onBack: () -> Unit,
    viewModel: CompatibilityViewModel = hiltViewModel()
) {
    val state by viewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    
    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            TopAppBar(
                title = { Text("Zodiac Compatibility 💑") },
                navigationIcon = {
                    IconButton(onClick = onBack) {
                        Icon(Icons.Default.ArrowBack, "Back", tint = Color.White)
                    }
                },
                colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(
                    containerColor = LunaColors.DeepBlack,
                    titleContentColor = LunaColors.Gold
                )
            )
        }
    ) { padding ->
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .background(LunaColors.DeepBlack)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(16.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            if (state.result == null) {
                Text("Your Sign", color = LunaColors.Gold, fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                ZodiacGrid(selected = state.sign1) { viewModel.onIntent(CompatibilityIntent.SelectSign1(it)) }
                
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))
                
                Text("Partner's Sign", color = LunaColors.Gold, fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                ZodiacGrid(selected = state.sign2) { viewModel.onIntent(CompatibilityIntent.SelectSign2(it)) }
                
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))
                
                Button(
                    onClick = { viewModel.onIntent(CompatibilityIntent.Calculate) },
                    enabled = state.sign1 != null && state.sign2 != null,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(56.dp),
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = LunaColors.Gold)
                ) {
                    Text("Calculate Compatibility 💕", color = Color.Black, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                }
            } else {
                CompatibilityResultView(state.result!!)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                Button(
                    onClick = { viewModel.onIntent(CompatibilityIntent.Reset) },
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = LunaColors.Gold)
                ) {
                    Text("Try Another", color = Color.Black)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ZodiacGrid(selected: ZodiacSign?, onSelect: (ZodiacSign) -> Unit) {
    // ✅ Sabit height - verticalScroll içinde crash önlendi
    LazyVerticalGrid(
        columns = GridCells.Fixed(4),
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(240.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
    ) {
        items(ZodiacSign.entries.size) { index ->
            val sign = ZodiacSign.entries[index]
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .aspectRatio(1f)
                    .clickable { onSelect(sign) }
                    .then(
                        if (selected == sign) Modifier.border(
                            2.dp, LunaColors.Gold, RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
                        ) else Modifier
                    ),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                    containerColor = if (selected == sign) LunaColors.Gold.copy(alpha = 0.2f) else LunaColors.SurfaceDark
                ),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
            ) {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(8.dp),
                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                ) {
                    Text(sign.symbol, fontSize = 24.sp)
                    Text(sign.displayName, fontSize = 10.sp, color = Color.White, textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun CompatibilityResultView(result: CompatibilityResult) {
    Card(

@Composable
private fun ScoreRow(label: String, score: Int) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 6.dp),

                color = LunaColors.Gold,
                trackColor = LunaColors.SurfaceVariant
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
            Text("$score%", color = LunaColors.Gold, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
        }
    }
}
```

### 📄 10. `feature/fortune/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/feature/fortune/angelnumbers/AngelNumbersViewModel.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.angelnumbers

import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.AngelNumber
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.AngelNumbersRepository
import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import javax.inject.Inject

@Immutable
data class AngelNumbersState(
    val allNumbers: List<AngelNumber> = emptyList(),
    val selectedNumber: AngelNumber? = null,
    val searchQuery: String = ""
)

sealed interface AngelNumbersIntent {
    data class Search(val query: String) : AngelNumbersIntent
    data class SelectNumber(val number: AngelNumber) : AngelNumbersIntent
    data object ClearSelection : AngelNumbersIntent
}

@HiltViewModel
class AngelNumbersViewModel @Inject constructor(
    private val repository: AngelNumbersRepository
) : ViewModel() {
    
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(AngelNumbersState())
    val state: StateFlow<AngelNumbersState> = _state.asStateFlow()
    
    init {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.getAllAngelNumbers().collect { numbers ->
                _state.update { it.copy(allNumbers = numbers) }
            }
        }
    }
    
    fun onIntent(intent: AngelNumbersIntent) {
        when (intent) {
            is AngelNumbersIntent.Search -> {
                _state.update { it.copy(searchQuery = intent.query) }
            }
            is AngelNumbersIntent.SelectNumber -> {
                _state.update { it.copy(selectedNumber = intent.number) }
            }
            is AngelNumbersIntent.ClearSelection -> {
                _state.update { it.copy(selectedNumber = null) }
            }
        }
    }
    
    fun getFilteredNumbers(): List<AngelNumber> {
        val query = _state.value.searchQuery
        return if (query.isBlank()) {
            _state.value.allNumbers
        } else {
            repository.searchAngelNumber(query)
        }
    }
}
```

```kotlin
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .background(LunaColors.DeepBlack)
                .padding(16.dp)
        ) {
            OutlinedTextField(
                value = state.searchQuery,
                onValueChange = { viewModel.onIntent(AngelNumbersIntent.Search(it)) },
                placeholder = { Text("Search (e.g. 111, 222...)") },
                leadingIcon = { Icon(Icons.Default.Search, null, tint = LunaColors.Gold) },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                colors = TextFieldDefaults.colors(
                    focusedTextColor = Color.White,
                    unfocusedTextColor = Color.White,
                    focusedContainerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark,
                    unfocusedContainerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark
                ),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
            )
            
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
            
            if (state.selectedNumber != null) {
                AngelNumberDetail(state.selectedNumber!!) {
                    viewModel.onIntent(AngelNumbersIntent.ClearSelection)
                }
            } else {
                // ✅ key eklendi - performans
                LazyColumn(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                    items(viewModel.getFilteredNumbers(), key = { it.number }) { number ->
                        AngelNumberCard(number) {
                            viewModel.onIntent(AngelNumbersIntent.SelectNumber(number))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AngelNumberCard(number: AngelNumber, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clickable(onClick = onClick),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Text(
                number.number,
                fontSize = 32.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = LunaColors.Gold,
                modifier = Modifier.width(80.dp)
            )
            Text(
                number.meaning.take(80) + "...",
                fontSize = 14.sp,
                color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.8f),
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AngelNumberDetail(number: AngelNumber, onBack: () -> Unit) {
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
    ) {
        TextButton(onClick = onBack) {
            Text("← Back to list", color = LunaColors.Gold)
        }
        
        Text(
            number.number,
            fontSize = 64.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            color = LunaColors.Gold,
            modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.CenterHorizontally)
        )
        
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
        
        DetailCard("✨ Meaning", number.meaning)
        DetailCard("💕 Love", number.loveMessage)
        DetailCard("💼 Career", number.careerMessage)
        DetailCard("🙏 Spiritual", number.spiritualMessage)
        DetailCard("🎯 Action", number.action)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun DetailCard(title: String, content: String) {
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 4.dp),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
            Text(title, color = LunaColors.Gold, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
            Text(content, color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.9f), fontSize = 14.sp, lineHeight = 22.sp)
        }
    }
}
```

### 📄 12. `feature/fortune/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/feature/fortune/luckynumbers/LuckyNumbersViewModel.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.luckynumbers

import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.ZodiacSign
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.LuckyNumbersRepository
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.LuckyNumbersResult
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.datastore.UserPreferencesDataStore
import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import javax.inject.Inject

@Immutable
data class LuckyNumbersState(
    val userSign: ZodiacSign = ZodiacSign.ARIES,
    val result: LuckyNumbersResult? = null
)

sealed interface LuckyNumbersIntent {
    data class ChangeSign(val sign: ZodiacSign) : LuckyNumbersIntent
    data object Refresh : LuckyNumbersIntent
}

@HiltViewModel
class LuckyNumbersViewModel @Inject constructor(
    private val repository: LuckyNumbersRepository,
    private val dataStore: UserPreferencesDataStore
) : ViewModel() {
    
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(LuckyNumbersState())
    val state: StateFlow<LuckyNumbersState> = _state.asStateFlow()
    
    init {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            dataStore.getUserZodiac().collect { zodiacName ->
                val sign = zodiacName?.let { 
                    ZodiacSign.entries.find { z -> z.name.equals(it, ignoreCase = true) } 
                } ?: ZodiacSign.ARIES
                _state.update { it.copy(userSign = sign) }
                refresh()
            }
        }
    }
    
    fun onIntent(intent: LuckyNumbersIntent) {
        when (intent) {
            is LuckyNumbersIntent.ChangeSign -> {
                _state.update { it.copy(userSign = intent.sign) }
                refresh()
            }
            is LuckyNumbersIntent.Refresh -> refresh()
        }
    }
    
    private fun refresh() {
        val result = repository.getDailyLuckyNumbers(_state.value.userSign)
        _state.update { it.copy(result = result) }
    }
}
```

### 📄 14. `feature/fortune/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/feature/fortune/affirmations/AffirmationsViewModel.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.affirmations

import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.Affirmation
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.AffirmationCategory
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.AffirmationRepository
import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import javax.inject.Inject

@Immutable
data class AffirmationsState(
    val selectedCategory: AffirmationCategory? = null,
    val dailyAffirmation: Affirmation? = null,
    val allAffirmations: List<Affirmation> = emptyList()
)

sealed interface AffirmationsIntent {
    data class SelectCategory(val category: AffirmationCategory?) : AffirmationsIntent
}

@HiltViewModel
class AffirmationsViewModel @Inject constructor(
    private val repository: AffirmationRepository
) : ViewModel() {
    
    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(
        AffirmationsState(dailyAffirmation = repository.getDailyAffirmation())
    )
    val state: StateFlow<AffirmationsState> = _state.asStateFlow()
    
    fun onIntent(intent: AffirmationsIntent) {
        when (intent) {
            is AffirmationsIntent.SelectCategory -> {
                val category = intent.category
                val affirmations = if (category == null) {
                    emptyList()
                } else {
                    repository.getAffirmationsByCategory(category)
                }
                _state.update { 
                    it.copy(selectedCategory = category, allAffirmations = affirmations) 
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.fortune.affirmations

import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)

                .fillMaxSize()

                        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark),

                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                        ) {
                            Text(category.icon, fontSize = 36.sp)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                            Text(
                                category.displayName,
                                color = Color.White,
                                fontSize = 14.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                                textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            
            if (state.selectedCategory != null) {
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                Text(
                    state.selectedCategory!!.displayName,
                    color = LunaColors.Gold,
                    fontSize = 16.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                LazyColumn(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(200.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                ) {
                    items(state.allAffirmations) { affirmation ->

                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
                        ) {

                                modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp),
                                color = Color.White,
                                fontSize = 14.sp,
                                lineHeight = 20.sp
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<resources>
    <string name="horoscope_aries">Cesur adımlar atmaktan korkma. Evren seni destekliyor.</string>

    <string name="horoscope_leo">Sahne senin. Yaratıcılığın parlıyor.</string>
    <string name="horoscope_virgo">Detaylara dikkat et. Mükemmeliyetçiliğin gücün.</string>
    <string name="horoscope_libra">Denge kur. İlişkilerde uyum zamanı.</string>
    <string name="horoscope_scorpio">Dönüşüm başlıyor. Eski kalıpları kır.</string>
    <string name="horoscope_sagittarius">Macera çağırıyor. Yeni deneyimlere açık ol.</string>
    <string name="horoscope_capricorn">Disiplinli çalış. Hedeflerine yaklaşıyorsun.</string>
    <string name="horoscope_aquarius">Yenilikçi fikirler ortaya çıkıyor.</string>
    <string name="horoscope_pisces">Sezgilerin keskin. İç sesini dinle.</string>
</resources>
```

### 📄 19. `feature/home/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/feature/home/HomeViewModel.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.home

import android.content.Context
import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.model.ZodiacSign
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.MysticCalendarRepository
import com.cynora.luna.core.database.datastore.UserPreferencesDataStore
import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
import dagger.hilt.android.qualifiers.ApplicationContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import javax.inject.Inject

@Immutable
data class HomeState(
    val moonPhase: String = "🌑 New Moon",
    val planetInfo: String = "☿ Mercury Direct",
    val dailyEnergy: String = "Transformation & Renewal",
    val luckyHour: String = "06:00",
    val zodiacSign: ZodiacSign = ZodiacSign.ARIES,
    val horoscopeText: String = "Today, don't be afraid to take bold steps.",
    val luckyNumber: Int = 7,
    val luckyColor: String = "Gold"
)

@HiltViewModel
class HomeViewModel @Inject constructor(
    @ApplicationContext private val context: Context,
    private val dataStore: UserPreferencesDataStore,
    private val calendarRepository: MysticCalendarRepository
) : ViewModel() {

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(HomeState())
    val state: StateFlow<HomeState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    init {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            dataStore.getUserZodiac().collect { zodiacName ->
                val zodiac = zodiacName?.let { 
                    ZodiacSign.entries.find { z -> z.name.equals(it, ignoreCase = true) } 
                } ?: ZodiacSign.ARIES
                
                _state.update { 
                    it.copy(
                        zodiacSign = zodiac,
                        horoscopeText = getHoroscopeForZodiac(zodiac),
                        luckyNumber = getLuckyNumber(zodiac),
                        luckyColor = getLuckyColor(zodiac)
                    ) 
                }
            }
        }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            calendarRepository.observeDailyData().collect { data ->
                _state.update { 
                    it.copy(
                        moonPhase = data.moonPhase,
                        planetInfo = data.planetInfo,
                        dailyEnergy = data.dailyEnergy,
                        luckyHour = data.luckyHour
                    ) 
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun getHoroscopeForZodiac(zodiac: ZodiacSign): String {
        // ✅ Feature modülünün kendi R'ı
        val resId = when (zodiac) {
            ZodiacSign.ARIES -> R.string.horoscope_aries
            ZodiacSign.TAURUS -> R.string.horoscope_taurus
            ZodiacSign.GEMINI -> R.string.horoscope_gemini
            ZodiacSign.CANCER -> R.string.horoscope_cancer
            ZodiacSign.LEO -> R.string.horoscope_leo
            ZodiacSign.VIRGO -> R.string.horoscope_virgo
            ZodiacSign.LIBRA -> R.string.horoscope_libra
            ZodiacSign.SCORPIO -> R.string.horoscope_scorpio
            ZodiacSign.SAGITTARIUS -> R.string.horoscope_sagittarius
            ZodiacSign.CAPRICORN -> R.string.horoscope_capricorn
            ZodiacSign.AQUARIUS -> R.string.horoscope_aquarius
            ZodiacSign.PISCES -> R.string.horoscope_pisces
        }
        return context.getString(resId)
    }

    private fun getLuckyNumber(zodiac: ZodiacSign): Int {
        return (zodiac.ordinal + 1) * 3 % 10 + 1
    }

    private fun getLuckyColor(zodiac: ZodiacSign): String {
        val colors = listOf("Gold", "Purple", "Blue", "Green", "Red", "White", "Silver")
        return colors[zodiac.ordinal % colors.size]
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.home

import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable

@Composable
fun HomeScreen(
    onNavigateToFortune: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToBirthChart: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToDailyCard: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToCompatibility: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToAngelNumbers: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToLuckyNumbers: () -> Unit,
    onNavigateToAffirmations: () -> Unit,
    viewModel: HomeViewModel = hiltViewModel()
) {
    val state by viewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val windowSize = windowSize()
    
    // ✅ Adaptive effects - cihaza göre optimize
    val starCount = adaptiveStarCount()
    val particleCount = adaptiveParticleCount()
    val nebulaEnabled = shouldEnableNebula()
    val shootingStarInterval = if (shouldEnableShootingStars()) 8000L else Long.MAX_VALUE
    
    CosmicBackground(
        starCount = starCount,
        particleCount = particleCount,
        nebulaEnabled = nebulaEnabled,
        shootingStarInterval = shootingStarInterval
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background(LunaColors.DeepBlack)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(
                    horizontal = when (windowSize) {
                        WindowSize.Compact -> 16.dp
                        WindowSize.Medium -> 24.dp
                        WindowSize.Expanded -> 48.dp
                    }
                )
        ) {
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(32.dp))

            Text(
                text = "Your Personal Cosmic Guide ✨",
                fontSize = 28.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = Color.White
            )

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

            Text(
                text = "Premium tarot, birth charts & offline fortune telling.",
                fontSize = 16.sp,
                color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.6f)
            )

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(32.dp))

            MysticCalendarWidget(
                moonPhase = state.moonPhase,
                planetInfo = state.planetInfo,
                dailyEnergy = state.dailyEnergy,
                luckyHour = state.luckyHour
            )

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))

            DailyHoroscopeCard(
                zodiacSign = state.zodiacSign.displayName,
                zodiacSymbol = state.zodiacSign.symbol,
                horoscopeText = state.horoscopeText,
                luckyNumber = state.luckyNumber,
                luckyColor = state.luckyColor
            )

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))

            Text(
                text = "Quick Actions",
                fontSize = 18.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                color = Color.White
            )

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {

                    QuickActionCard("Birth Chart", "🌌", Modifier.weight(1f), onNavigateToBirthChart)
                    QuickActionCard("Daily Card", "🎯", Modifier.weight(1f), onNavigateToDailyCard)

                    QuickActionCard("Angel Numbers", "🔮", Modifier.weight(1f), onNavigateToAngelNumbers)

                    QuickActionCard("Affirmations", "💫", Modifier.weight(1f), onNavigateToAffirmations)
                }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(32.dp))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun MysticCalendarWidget(
    moonPhase: String,
    planetInfo: String,
    dailyEnergy: String,
    luckyHour: String
) {
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = Color.Transparent)
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                .background(
                    brush = Brush.linearGradient(
                        colors = listOf(LunaColors.SurfaceDark, LunaColors.SurfaceVariant)
                    )
                )
                .padding(20.dp)
        ) {
            Column {
                Text(
                    text = "🌙 Mystic Calendar",
                    fontSize = 16.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                    color = LunaColors.Gold
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                CalendarInfoRow("🌕", "Moon Phase", moonPhase)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                CalendarInfoRow("🪐", "Planet", planetInfo)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                CalendarInfoRow("✨", "Energy", dailyEnergy)
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                CalendarInfoRow("⏰", "Lucky Hour", luckyHour)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun CalendarInfoRow(icon: String, label: String, value: String) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Text(
            text = icon,
            fontSize = 20.sp,
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(32.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(LunaColors.Gold.copy(alpha = 0.1f))
                .padding(6.dp),
            color = Color.White
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
        Column {
            Text(text = label, fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
            Text(text = value, fontSize = 14.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium, color = Color.White)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun DailyHoroscopeCard(
    zodiacSign: String,
    zodiacSymbol: String,
    horoscopeText: String,
    luckyNumber: Int,
    luckyColor: String
) {
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(20.dp)) {
            Text(
                text = "$zodiacSymbol $zodiacSign",
                fontSize = 18.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = LunaColors.Gold
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
            Text(
                text = horoscopeText,
                fontSize = 14.sp,
                lineHeight = 22.sp,
                color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.9f)
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceEvenly
            ) {
                HoroscopeStat("Lucky Number", luckyNumber.toString())
                HoroscopeStat("Lucky Color", luckyColor)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun RowScope.HoroscopeStat(label: String, value: String) {
    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f), horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
        Text(text = label, fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
        Text(text = value, fontSize = 16.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = Color.White)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun QuickActionCard(
    title: String,
    icon: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Card(
        modifier = modifier
            .height(100.dp)
            .clickable(onClick = onClick),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(16.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
        ) {
            Text(text = icon, fontSize = 32.sp)
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
            Text(
                text = title,
                fontSize = 14.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                color = Color.White
            )
        }
    }
}
```

### 📄 2. `feature/social/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/feature/social/SocialViewModel.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.social

import androidx.compose.runtime.Immutable
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.IntentionPost
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.SocialRepository
import dagger.hilt.android.lifecycle.HiltViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import timber.log.Timber
import javax.inject.Inject

@Immutable
data class SocialState(
    val posts: List<IntentionPost> = emptyList(),
    val isLoading: Boolean = true,
    val error: String? = null
)

sealed interface SocialIntent {
    data class PostIntention(val text: String) : SocialIntent
    data class LikePost(val postId: String, val currentLiked: Boolean) : SocialIntent
}

@HiltViewModel
class SocialViewModel @Inject constructor(
    private val repository: SocialRepository
) : ViewModel() {

    private val _state = MutableStateFlow(SocialState())
    val state: StateFlow<SocialState> = _state.asStateFlow()

    init {
        // ✅ ViewModel başlangıcında userId initialize et
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.ensureUserId()
            observePosts()
        }
    }

    private fun observePosts() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            repository.observePosts().collect { posts ->
                _state.update { it.copy(posts = posts, isLoading = false, error = null) }
            }
        }
    }

    fun onIntent(intent: SocialIntent) {
        when (intent) {
            is SocialIntent.PostIntention -> postIntention(intent.text)
            is SocialIntent.LikePost -> likePost(intent.postId, intent.currentLiked)
        }
    }

    // ✅ SYNC metod - Compose'dan çağrılabilir
    fun getCurrentUserIdSync(): String = repository.currentUserIdSync()

    private fun postIntention(text: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                val mysticNames = listOf(
                    "Stardust", "Moonlight", "Sunflower", "Crystal Heart",
                    "Blue Angel", "Golden Butterfly", "Silver Wolf", "Purple Lotus"
                )
                val avatarEmojis = listOf("🌟", "🌙", "☀️", "💎", "👼", "🦋", "🐺", "🪷")
                val nameIndex = (0 until mysticNames.size).random()
                
                val newPost = IntentionPost(
                    mysticName = mysticNames[nameIndex],
                    avatarEmoji = avatarEmojis[nameIndex],
                    intentionText = text,
                    timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis()
                )
                repository.addPost(newPost)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Timber.e(e, "Post intention failed")
                _state.update { it.copy(error = "Post failed") }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun likePost(postId: String, currentLiked: Boolean) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                val userId = repository.currentUserIdSync()
                
                // Optimistic update
                _state.update { currentState ->
                    currentState.copy(
                        posts = currentState.posts.map { post ->
                            if (post.id == postId) {
                                post.copy(
                                    likeCount = if (currentLiked) post.likeCount - 1 else post.likeCount + 1,
                                    likedBy = if (currentLiked) {
                                        post.likedBy - userId
                                    } else {
                                        post.likedBy + userId
                                    }
                                )
                            } else post
                        }
                    )
                }
                repository.toggleLike(postId)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Timber.e(e, "Like post failed")
                _state.update { it.copy(error = "Like failed") }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### 📄 3. `feature/social/src/main/java/com/cynora/luna/feature/social/SocialScreen.kt`

```kotlin
package com.cynora.luna.feature.social

import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement

import androidx.compose.material3.Text
import androidx.compose.material3.TextField
import androidx.compose.material3.TextFieldDefaults
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.remember
import androidx.compose.runtime.setValue
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier

import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color

import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import androidx.hilt.navigation.compose.hiltViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.collectAsStateWithLifecycle
import com.cynora.luna.core.data.repository.IntentionPost
import com.cynora.luna.core.ui.theme.LunaColors

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun SocialScreen(
    viewModel: SocialViewModel = hiltViewModel()
) {
    val state by viewModel.state.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    var intentionText by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(LunaColors.DeepBlack)
    ) {
        Text(
            text = "✨ Mystic Community",
            fontSize = 24.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            color = Color.White,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)
        )

        Card(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                TextField(
                    value = intentionText,
                    onValueChange = { intentionText = it },
                    placeholder = { Text("Write today's intention...", color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.4f)) },
                    colors = TextFieldDefaults.colors(
                        focusedContainerColor = Color.Transparent,
                        unfocusedContainerColor = Color.Transparent,
                        focusedTextColor = Color.White,
                        unfocusedTextColor = Color.White,
                        cursorColor = LunaColors.Gold
                    ),
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        if (intentionText.isNotBlank()) {
                            viewModel.onIntent(SocialIntent.PostIntention(intentionText))
                            intentionText = ""
                        }
                    },
                    enabled = intentionText.isNotBlank(),
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = LunaColors.Gold),
                    modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.End)
                ) {
                    Icon(Icons.Default.Send, contentDescription = "Send", modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                    Text("Share")
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

        if (state.isLoading) {
            Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                CircularProgressIndicator(color = LunaColors.Gold)
            }
        } else if (state.posts.isEmpty()) {
            Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                Text(
                    "No posts yet. Share your first intention ✨",
                    color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.6f),
                    fontSize = 14.sp
                )
            }
        } else {
            LazyColumn(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(16.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
            ) {
                items(state.posts, key = { it.id }) { post ->
                    IntentionPostCard(
                        post = post,
                        // ✅ SYNC çağrı - Compose'dan güvenli
                        currentUserId = viewModel.getCurrentUserIdSync(),
                        onLike = { liked ->
                            viewModel.onIntent(SocialIntent.LikePost(post.id, liked))
                        }
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun IntentionPostCard(
    post: IntentionPost,
    currentUserId: String,
    onLike: (Boolean) -> Unit
) {
    val isLiked = post.likedBy.contains(currentUserId)
    
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = LunaColors.SurfaceDark),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(40.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(LunaColors.Gold.copy(alpha = 0.2f)),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Text(text = post.avatarEmoji, fontSize = 20.sp)
                }

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

                Column {
                    Text(
                        text = post.mysticName,
                        fontSize = 14.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                        color = LunaColors.Gold
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = formatTimeAgo(post.timestamp),
                        fontSize = 12.sp,
                        color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.5f)
                    )
                }
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

            Text(
                text = "\"${post.intentionText}\"",
                fontSize = 16.sp,
                color = Color.White,
                lineHeight = 24.sp
            )

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
            ) {
                IconButton(onClick = { onLike(isLiked) }) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = if (isLiked) Icons.Default.Favorite else Icons.Default.FavoriteBorder,
                        contentDescription = "Like",
                        tint = if (isLiked) Color(0xFFE91E63) else Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.6f)
                    )
                }
                Text(
                    text = "${post.likeCount}",
                    fontSize = 14.sp,
                    color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.6f)
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

private fun formatTimeAgo(timestamp: Long): String {
    val diff = System.currentTimeMillis() - timestamp
    val minutes = diff / 60000
    val hours = minutes / 60
    val days = hours / 24
    
    return when {
        minutes < 1 -> "Just now"
        minutes < 60 -> "$minutes min ago"
        hours < 24 -> "$hours hours ago"
        else -> "$days days ago"
    }
}
```
